    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [option] })).toEqual({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [option] });
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, channelOrigin: 'ALIEN', policy: 'ALIEN' }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, policy: 'EVOLUTION' }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, supportedKinds: ['text', 'image'] }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, textConstraint: undefined }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, textConstraint: { unit: 'UTF8_BYTES', max: 999 } }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, textConstraint: { unit: 'UTF8_BYTES', max: 1000, extra: true } }] })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('validates the channel policy against its window contract while preserving unavailable null-window routes', () => {
    const pairs = [
      { channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', policy: 'INSTAGRAM_24H', requiresWindow: true },
      { channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', policy: 'OFFICIAL_WINDOW', requiresWindow: true },
      { channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', policy: 'EVOLUTION', requiresWindow: false },
    ];
    for (const pair of pairs) {
      const candidate = { ...option, ...pair, windowExpiresAt: pair.requiresWindow ? expiry : null,
        textConstraint: pair.channelOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM' ? { unit: 'UTF8_BYTES' as const, max: 1000 as const }
          : { unit: 'UNICODE_CODE_POINTS' as const, max: 4096 as const } };
      expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [candidate] })).not.toBeNull();
      expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...candidate, requiresWindow: !pair.requiresWindow }] })).toBeNull();
      const wrongConstraint = pair.channelOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM'
        ? { unit: 'UNICODE_CODE_POINTS', max: 4096 } : { unit: 'UTF8_BYTES', max: 1000 };
      expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...candidate, textConstraint: wrongConstraint }] })).toBeNull();
    }
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, available: false, reason: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY', windowOpen: false, windowExpiresAt: null }] })).not.toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, windowExpiresAt: null }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, windowExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000).toISOString() }] })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('accepts configured windowless official replies and keeps strict Instagram and Evolution policy flags', () => {
    const officialWindowless = { ...option, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', policy: 'OFFICIAL_WINDOW', requiresWindow: false,
      windowOpen: false, windowExpiresAt: null, textConstraint: { unit: 'UNICODE_CODE_POINTS' as const, max: 4096 as const } };
    const officialExpired = { ...option, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', policy: 'OFFICIAL_WINDOW', requiresWindow: true,
      windowExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000).toISOString(), textConstraint: { unit: 'UNICODE_CODE_POINTS' as const, max: 4096 as const } };
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [officialWindowless] })).not.toBeNull();
    expect(canUseReplyOption(officialWindowless, Date.now())).toBe(true);
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', requiresWindow: false }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...option, channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', policy: 'EVOLUTION', requiresWindow: true }] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [officialExpired] })).toBeNull();
    expect(safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [{ ...officialWindowless, windowExpiresAt: expiry }] })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('permits replies only for available text options inside the current window', () => {
    expect(canUseReplyOption(option, 1000)).toBe(true);
    expect(canUseReplyOption({ ...option, available: false }, 1000)).toBe(false);
    expect(canUseReplyOption({ ...option, supportedKinds: [] }, 1000)).toBe(false);
    expect(canUseReplyOption({ ...option, windowOpen: false }, 1000)).toBe(false);
    expect(canUseReplyOption({ ...option, windowExpiresAt: new Date(999).toISOString() }, 1000)).toBe(false);
    expect(canUseReplyOption({ ...option, windowExpiresAt: null }, Date.now())).toBe(false);
  });

  it('restores one usable route after explicit refresh but keeps ambiguous choices explicit', () => {
    expect(selectionAfterExplicitRefresh([option], Date.now())).toBe(replyOptionSelectionKey(option));
    expect(selectionAfterExplicitRefresh([{ ...option, available: false, reason: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' }], Date.now())).toBe('');
    expect(selectionAfterExplicitRefresh([option, { ...option, channelId: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222' }], Date.now())).toBe('');
    expect(selectionAfterExplicitRefresh([option, { ...option, channelId: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', available: false, reason: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' }], Date.now())).toBe('');
  });

  it('accepts only a safe callback UUID and a known error code', () => {
    expect(parseInstagramCallback(`?instagramAuthorization=${id}&code=private&state=private`).attemptId).toBe(id);
    expect(parseInstagramCallback('?instagramAuthorization=bad').attemptId).toBeNull();
    expect(parseInstagramCallback('?instagramAuthorizationError=OAUTH_STATE_INVALID').error).toBe('OAUTH_STATE_INVALID');
    expect(parseInstagramCallback('?instagramAuthorizationError=secret').error).toBeNull();
  });

  it('resumes only a UUID-bound persisted authorization hint', () => {
    expect(parseStoredInstagramAttempt(JSON.stringify({ id, buId: id, actorId: id }), id)).toEqual({ id, buId: id, actorId: id });
    expect(parseStoredInstagramAttempt(JSON.stringify({ id, buId: id, actorId: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222' }), id)).toBeNull();
    expect(parseStoredInstagramAttempt(JSON.stringify({ id: 'private', buId: id, actorId: id }), id)).toBeNull();
    expect(parseStoredInstagramAttempt('{', id)).toBeNull();
    expect(parseStoredInstagramAttempt(null, id)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('scopes prior read-only drafts to the same actor, BU and conversation and excludes active bindings', () => {
    let revision = 0;
    const next = () => ++revision;
    const old = tupleKey(id, id, id, id, id, 1, 'a'.repeat(64));
    const current = tupleKey(id, id, id, id, id, 2, 'b'.repeat(64));
    const otherActor = tupleKey('22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', id, id, id, id, 1, 'a'.repeat(64));
    const otherBu = tupleKey(id, '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333', id, id, id, 1, 'a'.repeat(64));
    const otherConversation = tupleKey(id, id, '44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444', id, id, 1, 'a'.repeat(64));
    const drafts = createDraftOwner();
    [old, current, otherActor, otherBu, otherConversation].forEach((key) => drafts.set(key, key === old ? 'rascunho antigo' : 'outro', next()));
    expect(drafts.forConversation(id, id, id, [current])).toEqual([{ key: old, text: 'rascunho antigo', revision: 1 }]);
  });

  it('indexes actual retained draft ownership by conversation for a 10,000-record cohort', () => {
    const drafts = createDraftOwner();
    let revision = 0;
    for (let index = 0; index < 10000; index += 1) {
      const actor = `actor-${index}`;
      const conversation = `conversation-${index}`;
      const key = tupleKey(actor, id, conversation, `route-${index}`, `channel-${index}`, 1, 'c'.repeat(64));
      drafts.set(key, `draft-${index}`, ++revision);
    }
    const target = tupleKey(id, id, id, 'route-target', 'channel-target', 1, 'd'.repeat(64));
    drafts.set(target, 'restore me', ++revision);
    expect(drafts.size()).toBe(10001);
    expect(drafts.forConversation(id, id, id)).toEqual([{ key: target, text: 'restore me', revision: 10001 }]);
    expect(drafts.forConversation('other-actor', id, id)).toEqual([]);
    const measuredOperations = 100;
    let scopedRowsReturned = 0;
    const startedAt = performance.now();
    for (let index = 0; index < measuredOperations; index += 1) {
      drafts.set(target, `measured-${index}`, ++revision);
      scopedRowsReturned += drafts.forConversation(id, id, id).length;
    }
    const elapsedMs = performance.now() - startedAt;
    const currentScope = drafts.forConversation(id, id, id);
    const measuredRecord = drafts.get(target)!;
    expect(drafts.size()).toBe(10001);
    expect(measuredRecord.revision).toBe(revision);
    expect(currentScope).toEqual([{ key: target, text: 'measured-99', revision: measuredRecord.revision }]);
    expect(drafts.forConversation('other-actor', id, id)).toEqual([]);
    console.info(`[DRAFT-OWNER-OBSERVATION]${JSON.stringify({ updates: measuredOperations, lookups: measuredOperations,
      elapsedMs, population: 10001, retainedCount: drafts.size(), currentScopeReturnedCount: currentScope.length,
      totalScopedRowsReturned: scopedRowsReturned })}`);
    const oldRevision = measuredRecord.revision;
    const updatedRevision = ++revision;
    drafts.set(target, 'updated', updatedRevision);
    expect(drafts.clearIfRevision(target, oldRevision)).toBe(false);
    expect(drafts.get(target)!.text).toBe('updated');
    expect(drafts.forConversation(id, id, id)).toEqual([{ key: target, text: 'updated', revision: updatedRevision }]);
  });

  it('uses distinct known channel labels and fixed safe outbound failure copy', () => {
    expect(channelOriginLabel('OFICIAL')).toBe('WhatsApp oficial');
    expect(channelOriginLabel('NAO_OFICIAL')).toBe('WhatsApp não oficial');
    expect(channelOriginLabel('INSTAGRAM')).toBe('Instagram');
    expect(channelOriginLabel('OTHER')).toBe('Canal');
    expect(outboundFailureCopy('OUTBOUND_FAILED')).toBe('Falha ao enviar mensagem.');
    expect(outboundFailureCopy(undefined, 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID')).toBe('O canal aceitou o envio sem confirmar o ID externo. Confira a conversa antes de tentar novamente.');
    expect(outboundFailureCopy('EXTERNAL_MESSAGE_ID_MISSING')).toBe('O canal aceitou o envio sem confirmar o ID externo. Confira a conversa antes de tentar novamente.');
  });
});


## FULL SOURCE BODY web-app/src/componentes/InstagramReplyComposer.tsx

import { FormEvent, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ErroDaApi, inboxApi, type ReplyOption } from '../api';
import { canUseReplyOption, channelOriginLabel, createActionLatch, createDraftOwner, createIntentClock, outboundFailureCopy, replyOptionSelectionKey, replyTextMeasurement, safeReplyOptions, selectionAfterExplicitRefresh, tupleKey } from '../instagramState';

const savedDrafts = createDraftOwner();
const scopesNeedingReview = new Set<string>();
let revision = 0;
const nextRevision = () => ++revision;
const sendLatches = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof createActionLatch>>();
function conversationLatch(actorId: string, buId: string, conversationId: string) {
  const scope = JSON.stringify([actorId, buId, conversationId]);
  let latch = sendLatches.get(scope);
  if (!latch) { latch = createActionLatch(); sendLatches.set(scope, latch); }
  return latch;
}

function replyFailure(cause: unknown): string {
  if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi) {
    if (cause.codigo === 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED' || cause.codigo === 'CHANNEL_GENERATION_STALE') {
      return 'A conexão ou conversa mudou. Atualize as opções antes de responder. O rascunho foi mantido.';
    }
    if (cause.codigo === 'JANELA_FECHADA') return 'A janela de resposta foi encerrada. O rascunho foi mantido.';
    if (cause.codigo === 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' || cause.codigo === 'CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE') {
      return 'Esta conexão não está pronta para responder. O rascunho foi mantido.';
    }
  }
  if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi) return outboundFailureCopy(cause.codigo, cause.evidence);
  return outboundFailureCopy();
}

function replyOptionReason(reason: string): string {
  const messages: Record<string, string> = {
    AVAILABLE: 'Disponível', JANELA_FECHADA: 'Janela de resposta encerrada', WINDOW_CLOSED: 'Janela de resposta encerrada',
    CHANNEL_NOT_READY: 'Conexão ainda não está pronta', CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE: 'Conexão indisponível',
    CHANNEL_IDENTITY_NOT_VERIFIED: 'Identidade da conexão precisa ser verificada', PENDING_CREDENTIAL: 'Credencial precisa ser atualizada',
    CANAL_AUSENTE: 'Nenhum canal conectado', CANAL_AMBIGUO: 'Canal ainda não definido',
  };
  return messages[reason] ?? 'Disponibilidade não confirmada';
}

export default function InstagramReplyComposer({ actorId, buId, conversationId, onSent, onOutcomeNeedsReview, admissionBlocked = false, metadataReconnectEpoch = 0, onAlert, onClearAlert }: {
  actorId: string; buId: string; conversationId: string; onSent: () => void; onOutcomeNeedsReview: () => Promise<void>; admissionBlocked?: boolean; metadataReconnectEpoch?: number;
  onAlert: (source: string, message: string) => number; onClearAlert: (source: string, version: number) => void;
}) {
  const reviewScope = JSON.stringify([actorId, buId, conversationId]);
  const [options, setOptions] = useState<ReplyOption[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState('');
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [optionsError, setOptionsError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reviewRequired, setReviewRequired] = useState(() => scopesNeedingReview.has(reviewScope));
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  const intent = useRef(createIntentClock());
  const optionsIntent = useRef(createIntentClock());
  const reconnectEpochRef = useRef(metadataReconnectEpoch);
  reconnectEpochRef.current = metadataReconnectEpoch;
  const failedOptionsReadEpoch = useRef(0);
  const attemptedReconnectReadEpoch = useRef(0);
  const alertVersions = useRef<Record<string, number>>({});
  const alertSources = useMemo(() => ({ send: `reply-send:${actorId}:${buId}:${conversationId}`, options: `reply-options:${actorId}:${buId}:${conversationId}` }), [actorId, buId, conversationId]);
  const mounted = useRef(true);
  const currentSelection = useRef({ value: '', version: 0 });
  function setSelection(value: string) {
    currentSelection.current = { value, version: currentSelection.current.version + 1 };
    setSelected(value);
  }
  const selectedOption = options.find((item) => replyOptionSelectionKey(item) === selected) ?? null;
  const optionUsable = canUseReplyOption(selectedOption, now);
  const textMeasurement = selectedOption ? replyTextMeasurement(text, selectedOption.textConstraint) : null;
  const textConstraintError = textMeasurement && !textMeasurement.validUnicode
    ? 'O texto contém um caractere Unicode incompleto. Corrija o rascunho antes de enviar; ele foi mantido.'
    : textMeasurement && !textMeasurement.withinLimit
      ? selectedOption?.textConstraint.unit === 'UTF8_BYTES'
        ? `A mensagem excede o limite de ${selectedOption.textConstraint.max} bytes do Instagram. Reduza o texto para enviar; o rascunho foi mantido.`
        : `A mensagem excede o limite de ${selectedOption?.textConstraint.max} caracteres deste canal. Reduza o texto para enviar; o rascunho foi mantido.`
      : null;
  const windowExpired = Boolean(selectedOption?.requiresWindow && (!selectedOption.windowOpen
    || (selectedOption.windowExpiresAt && Date.parse(selectedOption.windowExpiresAt) <= now)));
  const key = useMemo(() => selectedOption ? tupleKey(actorId, buId, conversationId, selectedOption.routeId,
    selectedOption.channelId, selectedOption.generation, selectedOption.contextFingerprint) : '',
  [actorId, buId, conversationId, selectedOption?.routeId, selectedOption?.channelId, selectedOption?.generation, selectedOption?.contextFingerprint]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (error) alertVersions.current.send = onAlert(alertSources.send, error);
    else if (alertVersions.current.send !== undefined) { onClearAlert(alertSources.send, alertVersions.current.send); delete alertVersions.current.send; }
  }, [error, alertSources.send, onAlert, onClearAlert]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (optionsError) alertVersions.current.options = onAlert(alertSources.options, optionsError);
    else if (alertVersions.current.options !== undefined) { onClearAlert(alertSources.options, alertVersions.current.options); delete alertVersions.current.options; }
  }, [optionsError, alertSources.options, onAlert, onClearAlert]);
  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (alertVersions.current.send !== undefined) onClearAlert(alertSources.send, alertVersions.current.send);
    if (alertVersions.current.options !== undefined) onClearAlert(alertSources.options, alertVersions.current.options);
  }, [alertSources.send, alertSources.options, onClearAlert]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    const ticket = optionsIntent.current.next();
    const readReconnectEpoch = reconnectEpochRef.current;
    setLoading(true); setError(null); setOptionsError(null); setNotice(null); setOptions([]); setSelection(''); setText('');
    inboxApi.replyOptions(conversationId).then((raw) => {
      const safe = safeReplyOptions(raw);
      if (!safe) throw new Error('As opções de resposta mudaram. Atualize a conversa antes de responder.');
      if (!mounted.current || !optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) return;
      setOptions(safe.options);
      setOptionsError(null);
      setSelection(selectionAfterExplicitRefresh(safe.options, Date.now()));
      if (!safe.options.some((item) => canUseReplyOption(item, Date.now()))) setNotice(safe.options.length ? 'As opções atuais não permitem responder. Confira o motivo informado em cada rota.' : 'Nenhuma rota de resposta está disponível para esta conversa.');
    }).catch(() => {
      if (!mounted.current || !optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) return;
      failedOptionsReadEpoch.current = readReconnectEpoch;
      setOptionsError('Não foi possível carregar as opções de resposta. Atualize a conversa antes de responder.');
    }).finally(() => { if (mounted.current && optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) setLoading(false); });
    return () => { mounted.current = false; optionsIntent.current.next(); intent.current.next(); };
  }, [actorId, buId, conversationId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!metadataReconnectEpoch || !optionsError || loading || !mounted.current
      || metadataReconnectEpoch <= failedOptionsReadEpoch.current
      || metadataReconnectEpoch <= attemptedReconnectReadEpoch.current) return;
    const ticket = optionsIntent.current.next();
    const readReconnectEpoch = metadataReconnectEpoch;
    attemptedReconnectReadEpoch.current = readReconnectEpoch;
    const selectionVersion = currentSelection.current.version;
    inboxApi.replyOptions(conversationId).then((raw) => {
      const safe = safeReplyOptions(raw);
      if (!safe) throw new Error('invalid reply options');
      if (!mounted.current || !optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket) || currentSelection.current.version !== selectionVersion) return;
      setOptions(safe.options);
      setOptionsError(null);
    }).catch(() => {
      if (!mounted.current || !optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) return;
      failedOptionsReadEpoch.current = readReconnectEpoch;
      if (currentSelection.current.version === selectionVersion) setOptionsError('Não foi possível carregar as opções de resposta. Atualize a conversa antes de responder.');
    });
  }, [metadataReconnectEpoch, optionsError, loading, conversationId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!key) return;
    const restored = savedDrafts.get(key)?.text ?? '';
    savedDrafts.set(key, restored, nextRevision(), selectedOption?.textConstraint.unit === 'UTF8_BYTES' ? null : 4096);
    setText(restored);
  }, [key, selectedOption?.textConstraint.unit]);

  function changeText(value: string) {
    const wholeTextIsRequired = selectedOption?.textConstraint.unit === 'UTF8_BYTES';
    const bounded = wholeTextIsRequired ? value : [...value].slice(0, 4096).join('');
    setText(bounded);
    if (key) savedDrafts.set(key, bounded, nextRevision(), wholeTextIsRequired ? null : 4096);
  }

  async function refreshOptions() {
    const ticket = optionsIntent.current.next();
    const readReconnectEpoch = reconnectEpochRef.current;
    setLoading(true); setSelection(''); setNotice(null); setReviewRequired(true);
    try {
      await onOutcomeNeedsReview();
      const safe = safeReplyOptions(await inboxApi.replyOptions(conversationId));
      if (!safe) throw new Error('options changed');
      if (!mounted.current || !optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) return;
      setOptions(safe.options);
      setOptionsError(null);
      setSelection(selectionAfterExplicitRefresh(safe.options, Date.now()));
      setReviewRequired(false);
      scopesNeedingReview.delete(reviewScope);
      if (!safe.options.length) setNotice('Nenhuma rota de resposta está disponível para esta conversa.');
      else if (safe.options.every((item) => !item.available)) setNotice('As opções atuais não permitem responder. Confira o motivo informado em cada rota.');
    } catch {
      if (mounted.current && optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) {
        failedOptionsReadEpoch.current = readReconnectEpoch;
        setOptionsError('Não foi possível confirmar o estado atual. O rascunho foi mantido; atualize as opções antes de responder.');
      }
    } finally { if (mounted.current && optionsIntent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) setLoading(false); }
  }

  async function send(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const option = selectedOption;
    const capturedSelection = option ? replyOptionSelectionKey(option) : '';
    const capturedSelectionVersion = currentSelection.current.version;
    const latch = conversationLatch(actorId, buId, conversationId);
    const actionTicket = latch.acquire();
    if (actionTicket === null || admissionBlocked || !option || currentSelection.current.value !== capturedSelection || !text.trim() || !optionUsable
      || !textMeasurement?.validUnicode || !textMeasurement.withinLimit || reviewRequired
      || !safeReplyOptions({ schemaVersion: 2, options: [option] })) {
      if (actionTicket !== null) latch.release(actionTicket);
      return;
    }
    const ticket = intent.current.current();
    const capturedKey = key;
    const capturedText = text;
    const capturedRevision = savedDrafts.get(capturedKey)?.revision;
    setSending(true); setError(null); setNotice(null);
    try {
      await inboxApi.reply(conversationId, { texto: capturedText, schemaVersion: 2, routeId: option.routeId,
        channelId: option.channelId, expectedGeneration: option.generation, contextFingerprint: option.contextFingerprint });
      if (!mounted.current || !intent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) return;
      const stillOwnsSelection = currentSelection.current.value === capturedSelection && currentSelection.current.version === capturedSelectionVersion;
      if (stillOwnsSelection && capturedRevision !== undefined && savedDrafts.get(capturedKey)?.revision === capturedRevision) {
        savedDrafts.clearIfRevision(capturedKey, capturedRevision);
        setText('');
      }
      if (stillOwnsSelection) setNotice('Resposta enviada.');
      onSent();
    } catch (cause) {
      if (mounted.current && intent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) {
        scopesNeedingReview.add(reviewScope);
        setReviewRequired(true);
        const stillOwnsSelection = currentSelection.current.value === capturedSelection && currentSelection.current.version === capturedSelectionVersion;
        if (stillOwnsSelection) { setSelection(''); setError(replyFailure(cause)); }
        else if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && (cause.codigo === 'EXTERNAL_MESSAGE_ID_MISSING' || cause.evidence === 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID')) setError(replyFailure(cause));
        else setNotice('Um envio anterior não foi confirmado. Atualize as opções antes de responder novamente.');
        void onOutcomeNeedsReview().catch(() => undefined);
      }
    } finally {
      latch.release(actionTicket);
      if (mounted.current && intent.current.isCurrent(ticket)) setSending(false);
    }
  }

  return <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Responder conversa">
    {admissionBlocked && <span role="status">O envio anterior permanece em confirmação. Nenhuma nova resposta pode ser enviada.</span>}
    <InstagramDraftReview actorId={actorId} buId={buId} conversationId={conversationId} activeOptions={selectedOption ? [selectedOption] : []} />
    {loading ? <p role="status">Conferindo rotas de resposta…</p> : <form className="grade" onSubmit={(event) => void send(event)}>
      {options.length > 1 && <label>Canal e conta
        <select aria-label="Canal e conta" value={selected} onChange={(event) => setSelection(event.target.value)} disabled={loading || reviewRequired}>
          <option value="">Escolha uma rota</option>
          {options.map((item) => <option key={replyOptionSelectionKey(item)} value={replyOptionSelectionKey(item)} disabled={!item.available || !item.supportedKinds.includes('text')}>
            {item.accountLabel || 'Conexão'} · {channelOriginLabel(item.channelOrigin)} · {replyOptionReason(item.reason)}
          </option>)}
        </select>
      </label>}
      {options.length === 1 && <p className="suave">{options[0].accountLabel} · {channelOriginLabel(options[0].channelOrigin)} · {replyOptionReason(options[0].reason)}</p>}
      <label>Resposta
        <textarea value={text} onChange={(event) => changeText(event.target.value)}
          disabled={admissionBlocked || !optionUsable || loading || reviewRequired} placeholder="Escreva uma resposta" />
      </label>
      {textConstraintError && <span role="status">{textConstraintError}</span>}
      {windowExpired && <span role="status">A janela de resposta deste canal está fechada.</span>}
      {notice && <span role="status">{notice}</span>}
      {!loading && <button type="button" disabled={sending} onClick={() => void refreshOptions()}>Atualizar opções</button>}
      <button className="primario" disabled={admissionBlocked || !optionUsable || !text.trim() || Boolean(textConstraintError) || sending || loading || reviewRequired}>
        {sending ? 'Enviando…' : 'Enviar'}
      </button>
    </form>}
  </section>;
}

export function InstagramDraftReview({ actorId, buId, conversationId, activeOptions = [] }: {
  actorId: string; buId: string; conversationId: string; activeOptions?: ReplyOption[];
}) {
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const activeKeys = activeOptions.map((option) => tupleKey(actorId, buId, conversationId, option.routeId,
    option.channelId, option.generation, option.contextFingerprint));
  const drafts = savedDrafts.forConversation(actorId, buId, conversationId, activeKeys);
  if (!drafts.length) return null;
  return <details className="aviso grade">
    <summary>Revisar rascunhos de conexões anteriores ({drafts.length})</summary>
    <p className="suave">Estes textos são somente leitura e não serão transferidos para uma conexão atual. Confira a conversa antes de reutilizá-los.</p>
    {drafts.map((draft, index) => <div className="grade" key={draft.key}>
      <label>{`Rascunho anterior ${index + 1}`}
        <textarea readOnly value={draft.text} onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()} aria-label={`Rascunho anterior ${index + 1}`} />
      </label>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => {
        if (!navigator.clipboard?.writeText) { setMessage('Selecione o texto para copiá-lo manualmente.'); return; }
        void navigator.clipboard.writeText(draft.text).then(() => setMessage('Texto copiado para revisão manual.'))
          .catch(() => setMessage('Selecione o texto para copiá-lo manualmente.'));
      }}>Copiar para revisão manual</button>
    </div>)}
    {message && <span role="status">{message}</span>}
  </details>;
}


## FULL SOURCE BODY web-app/e2e/instagram-channel.spec.ts

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { expect, test, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { verifyInstagramFreshness } from './instagram-channel-freshness';

const FIXTURES = 'http://127.0.0.1:3101';
const FRONTDOOR = 'https://127.0.0.1:4174';
const FIXTURE_TOKEN = 'timeline-provider-port-only';

test.use({ baseURL: FRONTDOOR, ignoreHTTPSErrors: true });
test.beforeAll(async () => { await verifyInstagramFreshness(); });

type Fixture = { email: string; password: string; operatorEmail: string; operatorPassword: string; businessUnitId: string; conversationId: string };
type Incoming = { status: number; body: string; conversationId: string | null; messageId: string | null; timestamp: string | null };
type RealtimeFrame = { socketId: number; url: string; payload: string };

function isNativeRealtimeSocket(url: string) {
  const parsed = new URL(url);
  return parsed.protocol === 'wss:' && parsed.host === '127.0.0.1:4174' && parsed.pathname === '/ws';
}

function parseRealtimeFrame(frame: RealtimeFrame): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(frame.payload);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)
      ? parsed as Record<string, unknown>
      : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function hasRealtimeConnection(frames: RealtimeFrame[], socketId: number | null, buId: string) {
  return socketId !== null && frames.some((frame) => {
    if (frame.socketId !== socketId || !isNativeRealtimeSocket(frame.url)) return false;
    const event = parseRealtimeFrame(frame);
    return event?.tipo === 'conectado' && Array.isArray(event.escopo) && event.escopo.includes(buId);
  });
}

function countRealtimeIncoming(frames: RealtimeFrame[], socketId: number | null, conversationId: string, buId: string) {
  if (socketId === null) return 0;
  return frames.filter((frame) => {
    if (frame.socketId !== socketId || !isNativeRealtimeSocket(frame.url)) return false;
    const event = parseRealtimeFrame(frame);
    return event?.tipo === 'mensagem' && event.buId === buId
      && event.conversaId === conversationId && event.direcao === 'IN';
  }).length;
}

async function seed(request: APIRequestContext): Promise<Fixture> {
  const response = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/seed`, { data: { count: 3, includeEvolution: false, includeOperator: true } });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(201);
  return response.json();
}

async function fixturePost(request: APIRequestContext, path: string, data: unknown) {
  const response = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}${path}`, {
    headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': FIXTURE_TOKEN }, data,
  });
  return response;
}

async function fixtureGet(request: APIRequestContext, path: string) {
  return request.get(`${FIXTURES}${path}`, { headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': FIXTURE_TOKEN } });
}

async function login(page: Page, fixture: Fixture, operator = false) {
  await page.goto(`${FRONTDOOR}/login`);
  await page.getByLabel('E-mail').fill(operator ? fixture.operatorEmail : fixture.email);
  await page.getByLabel('Senha').fill(operator ? fixture.operatorPassword : fixture.password);
  const loginResponse = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && /\/api\/bff\/auth\/login$/.test(new URL(response.url()).pathname));
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' }).click();
  const response = await loginResponse;
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
  const cookies = await page.context().cookies(FRONTDOOR);
  expect(cookies.some((cookie) => cookie.httpOnly && cookie.secure && cookie.sameSite === 'None')).toBe(true);
}

async function connectAccount(page: Page, request: APIRequestContext, fixture: Fixture, account: 'a' | 'b') {
  const userName = account === 'a' ? 'timeline_account_a' : 'timeline_account_b';
  const code = `timeline-instagram-code-${account}`;
  await page.goto('/numeros');
  await page.unroute('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**');
  const section = page.locator('section[aria-label="Conexões de canais · Número timeline"]');
  const connect = section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Conectar conta Instagram' });
  await expect(connect).toBeEnabled();
  await page.route('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**', async (route) => {
    const authorization = new URL(route.request().url());
    const callback = authorization.searchParams.get('redirect_uri');
    const state = authorization.searchParams.get('state');
    expect(callback).toBeTruthy();
    expect(state).toBeTruthy();
    const callbackUrl = new URL(callback!);
    callbackUrl.searchParams.set('code', code);
    callbackUrl.searchParams.set('state', state!);
    await route.fulfill({ status: 200, contentType: 'text/html', body: `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><script>location.replace(${JSON.stringify(callbackUrl.toString())})</script>` });
  });
  const externalAuthorizationRequest = page.waitForRequest((outgoing) => {
    const url = new URL(outgoing.url());
    return url.hostname === 'www.instagram.com' && url.pathname === '/oauth/authorize';
  });
  const callback = page.waitForResponse((response) => new URL(response.url()).pathname === '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback');
  await connect.click();
  const authorizationRequest = await externalAuthorizationRequest;
  const authorizationUrl = new URL(authorizationRequest.url());
  expect(authorizationUrl.searchParams.get('client_id')).toBe('timeline-instagram-client');
  expect(authorizationUrl.searchParams.get('response_type')).toBe('code');
  expect(authorizationUrl.searchParams.get('redirect_uri')).toBe('https://127.0.0.1:4174/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback');
  expect(authorizationUrl.searchParams.get('state')).toBeTruthy();
  const callbackResponse = await callback;
  expect(callbackResponse.status()).toBe(303);
  expect(callbackResponse.headers()['cache-control']).toContain('no-store');
  expect(callbackResponse.headers()['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
  await expect(section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirmar conta' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/numeros$/);
  const confirmationResponse = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
    && /^\/api\/bff\/instagram-authorizations\/[^/]+\/confirm$/.test(new URL(response.url()).pathname));
  await section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirmar conta' }).click();
  const confirmed = await confirmationResponse;
  expect(confirmed.status()).toBe(200);
  expect(await confirmed.finished()).toBeNull();
  const attemptsResponse = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/attempts', {
    buId: fixture.businessUnitId, accountId: `timeline-ig-account-${account}`,
  });
  expect(attemptsResponse.status()).toBe(200);
  const { attempts } = await attemptsResponse.json();
  expect(attempts).toHaveLength(1);
  expect(attempts[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT', username: userName, subscriptionState: 'SUBSCRIBED', subscriptionRequests: 1 });
  expect(JSON.stringify(attempts)).not.toMatch(/code|token|stateHash|credentialCiphertext/i);

  const accountCard = section.locator('article').filter({ hasText: `@${userName}` });
  await expect(accountCard.locator('strong')).toHaveText(`@${userName}`);
  await expect(accountCard.getByRole('status')).toContainText('Conexão em andamento');
  await expect(accountCard.getByText(/recebimento/i)).toBeVisible();
  await page.waitForTimeout(10_500);
  await accountCard.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verificar conexão' }).click();
  await expect(section.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'A verificação da conta ainda não foi confirmada' })).toBeVisible();
  const provider = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
  expect(provider.calls.filter((item: { operation: string; accountId: string }) => item.accountId === `timeline-ig-account-${account}` && item.operation === 'subscription.get')).toHaveLength(1);
  expect(provider.calls.filter((item: { operation: string; accountId: string }) => item.accountId === `timeline-ig-account-${account}` && item.operation === 'subscription.post')).toHaveLength(1);
  const beforeReceipt = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/observe', { accountId: `timeline-ig-account-${account}` })).json();
  expect(beforeReceipt).toMatchObject({ connected: true, routes: 0, qualifiedInbound: 0, providerIdentity: true, receiptVerifiedAt: null });
  return attempts[0] as { id: string };
}

async function signedIncoming(request: APIRequestContext, account: 'a' | 'b', text: string, peerId?: string): Promise<Incoming> {
  const response = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/incoming', {
    accountId: `timeline-ig-account-${account}`, text, ...(peerId ? { peerId } : {}),
  });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
  const result = await response.json() as Incoming;
  expect(result.status).toBe(200);
  expect(result.conversationId).toBeTruthy();
  expect(result.messageId).toBeTruthy();
  expect(result.timestamp).toBeTruthy();
  return result;
}

async function assertInstagramFakeCredentialBoundaries(request: APIRequestContext) {
  const before = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
  const rejectedExchange = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/request', {
    url: 'https://api.instagram.com/oauth/access_token', method: 'POST',
    contentType: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
    body: new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: 'timeline-instagram-client', client_secret: 'timeline-instagram-client-secret',
      grant_type: 'authorization_code', redirect_uri: 'https://127.0.0.1:4174/wrong-callback',
      code: 'timeline-instagram-code-a',
    }).toString(),
  });
  expect(rejectedExchange.status()).toBe(400);
  const rejectedSubscription = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/request', {
    url: 'https://graph.instagram.com/v26.0/timeline-ig-account-a/subscribed_apps?access_token=timeline-ig-access:timeline-ig-account-b',
    method: 'GET', contentType: null, authorization: null, body: null,
  });
  expect(rejectedSubscription.status()).toBe(403);
  const rejectedSend = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/request', {
    url: 'https://graph.instagram.com/v26.0/timeline-ig-account-a/messages', method: 'POST',
    contentType: 'application/json', authorization: 'Bearer timeline-ig-access:timeline-ig-account-b',
    body: JSON.stringify({ recipient: { id: 'timeline-ig-peer' }, message: { text: 'rejeitada sem credencial da conta' } }),
  });
  expect(rejectedSend.status()).toBe(403);
  const after = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
  expect(after.calls).toEqual(before.calls);
  expect(after.effects).toEqual(before.effects);
}

async function hold(request: APIRequestContext, conversationId: string) {
  const response = await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/hold`, { data: { conversationId } });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
}

async function waitForNativeHold(request: APIRequestContext) {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 200; attempt += 1) {
    const response = await request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/state`);
    expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
    const state = await response.json();
    if (state.pending) return state.pending as { status: number; bodyHash: string; bodyBytes: number; headers: Record<string, string> };
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 25));
  }
  throw new Error('The native HTTPS proxy did not hold the completed BFF response.');
}

test.describe('Instagram real HTTPS and BFF browser transport', () => {
  test('enforces the real reply-option UTF-8 constraint at byte boundaries without clipping drafts', async ({ page, request }) => {
    test.setTimeout(180_000);
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'a');
    const incoming = await signedIncoming(request, 'a', 'entrada para limites UTF-8');
    await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/config', { sendMode: 'accepted' });
    await page.goto('/inbox');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    const thread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: '@timeline_account_a' }).filter({ hasText: 'fixture_peer' });
    await expect(thread).toBeVisible();
    const optionsPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/reply-options`;
    const optionsWait = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === optionsPath);
    await thread.click();
    const optionsResponse = await optionsWait;
    expect(optionsResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const replyOptions = await optionsResponse.json() as { options: Array<{
      routeId: string; channelId: string; generation: number; contextFingerprint: string;
      channelOrigin: string; textConstraint: { unit: string; max: number };
    }> };
    const route = replyOptions.options.find((item) => item.channelOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM');
    expect(route).toMatchObject({ textConstraint: { unit: 'UTF8_BYTES', max: 1000 } });
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    const send = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true });
    await expect(composer).toBeVisible();

    const valid = [
      'a'.repeat(1000),
      'é'.repeat(500),
      '€'.repeat(333) + 'a',
      '😀'.repeat(250),
      'a'.repeat(997) + '€',
      ' ' + '　' + 'a'.repeat(992) + '　' + ' ',
    ];
    let browserReplyPosts = 0;
    page.on('request', (browserRequest) => {
      if (browserRequest.method() === 'POST' && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`) browserReplyPosts += 1;
    });
    const providerBeforeValid = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    const sendsBeforeValid = providerBeforeValid.effects.filter((effect: { operation: string; accountId: string; peerId: string }) =>
      effect.operation === 'message.send' && effect.accountId === 'timeline-ig-account-a' && effect.peerId === 'timeline-ig-peer').length;
    for (const text of valid) {
      expect(new TextEncoder().encode(text).byteLength).toBe(1000);
      await composer.fill(text);
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
      await expect(send).toBeEnabled();
      const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`);
      await send.click();
      const response = await responsePromise;
      expect(response.status()).toBe(201);
      expect(response.request().postDataJSON()).toMatchObject({ texto: text, routeId: route!.routeId, channelId: route!.channelId,
        expectedGeneration: route!.generation, contextFingerprint: route!.contextFingerprint, schemaVersion: 2 });
      expect(Object.keys(response.request().postDataJSON()).sort()).toEqual(['channelId', 'contextFingerprint', 'expectedGeneration', 'routeId', 'schemaVersion', 'texto']);
      const createdMessage = await response.json() as { id: string };
      expect(createdMessage.id).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i);
      await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: text })).toBeVisible();
      const createdBubble = page.locator(`.bolha[data-message-id="${createdMessage.id}"]`);
      await expect(createdBubble).toBeVisible();
      await expect.poll(() => createdBubble.locator(':scope > div').first().textContent()).toBe(text);
    }
    const providerAfterValid = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    const sendsAfterValid = providerAfterValid.effects.filter((effect: { operation: string; accountId: string; peerId: string }) =>
      effect.operation === 'message.send' && effect.accountId === 'timeline-ig-account-a' && effect.peerId === 'timeline-ig-peer').length;
    expect(sendsAfterValid).toBe(sendsBeforeValid + valid.length);
    expect(providerAfterValid.messageRequests.slice(-valid.length)).toEqual(valid.map((text: string) => ({
      accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', peerId: 'timeline-ig-peer', text,
    })));
    expect(browserReplyPosts).toBe(valid.length);
    const bubblesAfterValid = await page.locator('.bolha').count();
    const startOptionsPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/start-options`;
    const admissionsBeforeInvalidResponse = await page.context().request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}${startOptionsPath}`, {
      headers: { 'x-requested-with': 'leadshug-web' },
    });
    expect(admissionsBeforeInvalidResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const admissionsBeforeInvalid = (await admissionsBeforeInvalidResponse.json() as { ordinaryAdmissions: unknown[] }).ordinaryAdmissions;

    const invalid = [
      'a'.repeat(1001),
      'é'.repeat(501),
      '€'.repeat(334),
      '😀'.repeat(251),
      'a'.repeat(998) + '😀',
      'a'.repeat(997) + '😀',
      ' ' + '　' + 'a'.repeat(993) + '　' + ' ',
    ];
    const providerBefore = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    const sendsBefore = providerBefore.effects.filter((effect: { operation: string; accountId: string; peerId: string }) =>
      effect.operation === 'message.send' && effect.accountId === 'timeline-ig-account-a' && effect.peerId === 'timeline-ig-peer').length;
    for (const text of invalid) {
      expect(new TextEncoder().encode(text).byteLength).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
      await composer.fill(text);
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
      await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'excede o limite de 1000 bytes do Instagram' })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(send).toBeDisabled();
      const rejected = await page.context().request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`, {
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-requested-with': 'leadshug-web' },
        data: { texto: text, schemaVersion: 2, routeId: route!.routeId, channelId: route!.channelId,
          expectedGeneration: route!.generation, contextFingerprint: route!.contextFingerprint },
      });
      expect(rejected.status()).toBe(400);
      expect((await rejected.json()).code).toBe('TEXTO_LONGO');
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
    }
    const providerAfter = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    expect(providerAfter.effects.filter((effect: { operation: string; accountId: string; peerId: string }) =>
      effect.operation === 'message.send' && effect.accountId === 'timeline-ig-account-a' && effect.peerId === 'timeline-ig-peer')).toHaveLength(sendsBefore);
    expect(providerAfter.messageRequests).toHaveLength(providerBefore.messageRequests.length);
    const admissionsAfterInvalidResponse = await page.context().request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}${startOptionsPath}`, {
      headers: { 'x-requested-with': 'leadshug-web' },
    });
    expect(admissionsAfterInvalidResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await admissionsAfterInvalidResponse.json() as { ordinaryAdmissions: unknown[] }).ordinaryAdmissions).toEqual(admissionsBeforeInvalid);
    expect(browserReplyPosts).toBe(valid.length);
    expect(await page.locator('.bolha').count()).toBe(bubblesAfterValid);
  });

  test('automatically recovers a held uncertain-send metadata read after a real BU websocket reconnect', async ({ page, request }) => {
    test.setTimeout(180_000);
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'a');
    const incoming = await signedIncoming(request, 'a', 'entrada para recuperação após reconexão');
    await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/config', { sendMode: 'unknown' });

    const connectedSockets = new Set<import('@playwright/test').WebSocket>();
    const closedSockets = new Set<import('@playwright/test').WebSocket>();
    page.on('websocket', (socket) => {
      if (socket.url() !== 'wss://127.0.0.1:4174/ws') return;
      socket.on('close', () => closedSockets.add(socket));
      socket.on('framereceived', (frame) => {
        try {
          const event: unknown = JSON.parse(typeof frame.payload === 'string' ? frame.payload : frame.payload.toString());
          if (event && typeof event === 'object' && !Array.isArray(event)
            && (event as { tipo?: unknown }).tipo === 'conectado'
            && Array.isArray((event as { escopo?: unknown }).escopo)
            && (event as { escopo: string[] }).escopo.includes(fixture.businessUnitId)) connectedSockets.add(socket);
        } catch { /* Ignore non-JSON websocket frames. */ }
      });
    });

    await page.goto('/inbox');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    await expect.poll(() => connectedSockets.size).toBe(1);
    const thread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: 'fixture_peer' });
    await expect(thread).toBeVisible();
    await thread.click();
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
    const startOptionsPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/start-options`;
    let messagePosts = 0;
    page.on('request', (browserRequest) => {
      if (browserRequest.method() === 'POST' && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`) messagePosts += 1;
    });
    let heldReadSeen = false;
    let continuedReads = 0;
    let resolveHeldRead!: () => void;
    const heldRead = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { resolveHeldRead = resolve; });
    let abortHeldRead!: () => void;
    const abortSignal = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { abortHeldRead = resolve; });
    let finishHeldHandler!: () => void;
    let heldHandlerFinished: Promise<void> | null = null;
    const optionsRoute = `**${startOptionsPath}`;
    const optionsHandler = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      if (!heldReadSeen) {
        heldReadSeen = true;
        heldHandlerFinished = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { finishHeldHandler = resolve; });
        resolveHeldRead();
        try {
          await abortSignal;
          await route.abort('failed');
        } catch (error) {
          if (route.request().failure()?.errorText !== 'net::ERR_ABORTED') throw error;
        } finally {
          finishHeldHandler();
        }
        return;
      }
      continuedReads += 1;
      await route.continue();
    };
    await page.route(optionsRoute, optionsHandler);

    let heldReadReleased = false;
    try {
      const text = 'resposta mantida durante a reconexão do canal';
      await composer.fill(text);
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
      const outbound = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true }).click();
      const outboundResponse = await outbound;
      expect(outboundResponse.status()).toBe(409);
      await heldRead;
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);

      const providerBeforeDrop = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
      expect(providerBeforeDrop.effects.filter((effect: { operation: string; accountId: string; peerId: string }) =>
        effect.operation === 'message.send' && effect.accountId === 'timeline-ig-account-a' && effect.peerId === 'timeline-ig-peer')).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(providerBeforeDrop.messageRequests).toContainEqual({ accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', peerId: 'timeline-ig-peer', text });

      const socketDrop = await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/drop-wss`, {
        headers: { 'x-fixture-wss-control': 'native109-raw-wss-close-only' },
      });
      expect(socketDrop.status()).toBe(200);
      expect(await socketDrop.json()).toEqual({ closed: 1 });
      await expect.poll(() => closedSockets.size).toBe(1);
      await expect.poll(() => connectedSockets.size, { timeout: 15_000 }).toBeGreaterThan(1);

      const metadataFailure = page.waitForEvent('requestfailed', (browserRequest) => browserRequest.method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === startOptionsPath);
      const recoveredRead = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === startOptionsPath);
      abortHeldRead();
      heldReadReleased = true;
      await metadataFailure;
      const recoveredResponse = await recoveredRead;
      expect(recoveredResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      expect(await recoveredResponse.finished()).toBeNull();
      const options = await recoveredResponse.json() as { ordinaryAdmissions: Array<{ id: string; state: string }> };
      expect(options.ordinaryAdmissions.some((item) => item.state === 'UNCERTAIN')).toBe(true);
      await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recuperação do envio comum' })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
      await expect(composer).toBeDisabled();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true })).toBeDisabled();
      await expect(page.getByText('O envio continua bloqueado até uma leitura atual.')).toHaveCount(0);
      expect(continuedReads).toBe(1);
      expect(messagePosts).toBe(1);

      const providerAfterRecovery = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
      expect(providerAfterRecovery.effects).toEqual(providerBeforeDrop.effects);
      expect(providerAfterRecovery.messageRequests).toEqual(providerBeforeDrop.messageRequests);
    } finally {
      if (!heldReadReleased) {
        abortHeldRead();
        heldReadReleased = true;
      }
      if (heldHandlerFinished) await heldHandlerFinished;
      await page.unroute(optionsRoute, optionsHandler);
    }
  });

  test('keeps an active ordinary admission blocked when its metadata read fails, then recovers on an explicit real read', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'a');
    const incoming = await signedIncoming(request, 'a', 'entrada para admissão incerta');
    await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/config', { sendMode: 'unknown' });
    await page.goto('/inbox');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    const thread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: 'fixture_peer' });
    await expect(thread).toBeVisible();
    await thread.click();
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
    const startOptionsPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/start-options`;
    let failedMetadataRead = 0;
    const metadataRoute = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      failedMetadataRead += 1;
      await route.abort('failed');
    };
    await page.route(`**${startOptionsPath}`, metadataRoute);
    try {
      await composer.fill('😀'.repeat(1000));
      expect(await composer.evaluate((element) => [...(element as HTMLTextAreaElement).value].length)).toBe(1000);
      await composer.fill('😀'.repeat(1001));
      expect(await composer.evaluate((element) => [...(element as HTMLTextAreaElement).value].length)).toBe(1001);
      await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'excede o limite de 1000 bytes do Instagram' })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true })).toBeDisabled();
      await composer.fill('resposta com estado de admissão incerto');
      const outbound = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`);
      const metadataFailure = page.waitForEvent('requestfailed', (browserRequest) => browserRequest.method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === startOptionsPath);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
      expect((await outbound).status()).toBe(409);
      await metadataFailure;
      expect(failedMetadataRead).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue('resposta com estado de admissão incerto');
      await expect(composer).toBeDisabled();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' })).toBeDisabled();
      let extraPosts = 0;
      page.on('request', (browserRequest) => {
        if (browserRequest.method() === 'POST' && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incoming.conversationId}/messages`) extraPosts += 1;
      });
      expect(extraPosts).toBe(0);

      await page.unroute(`**${startOptionsPath}`, metadataRoute);
      const recovered = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === startOptionsPath);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tentar novamente' }).click();
      const recoveredResponse = await recovered;
      expect(recoveredResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      const options = await recoveredResponse.json() as { ordinaryAdmissions: Array<{ id: string; state: string }> };
      expect(options.ordinaryAdmissions.some((item) => ['SENDING', 'UNCERTAIN'].includes(item.state))).toBe(true);
      await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recuperação do envio comum' })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue('resposta com estado de admissão incerto');
      await expect(composer).toBeDisabled();
      expect(extraPosts).toBe(0);
    } finally {
      await page.unroute(`**${startOptionsPath}`, metadataRoute);
    }
  });

  test('does not publish an older held start-options response after navigating to another conversation', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'a');
    const oldIncoming = await signedIncoming(request, 'a', 'entrada da conversa anterior', 'timeline-ig-page-peer-00');
    const currentIncoming = await signedIncoming(request, 'a', 'entrada da conversa atual', 'timeline-ig-page-peer-01');
    await page.goto('/inbox');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    const oldThread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: 'timeline-ig-page-peer-00' });
    const currentThread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: 'timeline-ig-page-peer-01' });
    await expect(oldThread).toBeVisible();
    await expect(currentThread).toBeVisible();
    let releaseOld: (() => void) | null = null;
    let signalHeld: (() => void) | null = null;
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { signalHeld = resolve; });
    const release = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseOld = resolve; });
    let fulfilledOld: (() => void) | null = null;
    const fulfilled = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { fulfilledOld = resolve; });
    const oldPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${oldIncoming.conversationId}/start-options`;
    let oldStatus = 0;
    const holdOld = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      const actual = await route.fetch();
      oldStatus = actual.status();
      signalHeld?.();
      await release;
      await route.fulfill({ response: actual });
      fulfilledOld?.();
    };
    await page.route(`**${oldPath}**`, holdOld);
    try {
      await oldThread.click();
      await held;
      const newOpen = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${currentIncoming.conversationId}/start-options`);
      await currentThread.click();
      const openedCurrent = await newOpen;
      expect(openedCurrent.status()).toBe(200);
      await expect(page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha > strong').first()).toHaveText('timeline-ig-page-peer-01');
      const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
      await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
      await expect(composer).toBeEnabled();
      releaseOld?.();
      await fulfilled;
      expect(oldStatus).toBe(200);
      await expect(page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha > strong').first()).toHaveText('timeline-ig-page-peer-01');
      await expect(composer).toBeEnabled();
      await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recuperação do envio comum' })).toHaveCount(0);
    } finally {
      releaseOld?.();
      await page.unroute(`**${oldPath}**`, holdOld);
    }
  });

  test('recovers lost authorization creation by replaying the same key and body from memory when storage is unavailable', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    await page.addInitScript(() => {
      Object.defineProperty(window, 'sessionStorage', { configurable: false, get: () => { throw new DOMException('synthetic storage denial', 'SecurityError'); } });
    });
    const createPath = `/api/bff/business-units/${fixture.businessUnitId}/instagram-authorizations`;
    const requests: Array<{ key: string; body: unknown }> = [];
    let durable: { id: string; status: number } | null = null;
    let createCount = 0;
    await page.route(`**${createPath}`, async (route) => {
      createCount += 1;
      const headers = route.request().headers();
      requests.push({ key: headers['idempotency-key'] ?? '', body: route.request().postDataJSON() });
      if (createCount === 1) {
        const response = await route.fetch();
        const body = await response.json() as { id: string };
        durable = { id: body.id, status: response.status() };
        await route.abort('failed');
        return;
      }
      await route.continue();
    });
    let authorizationRequests = 0;
    await page.route('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**', async (route) => {
      authorizationRequests += 1;
      await route.fulfill({ status: 200, contentType: 'text/html; charset=utf-8', body: '<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><p>Autorização aguardando retorno.</p>' });
    });
    try {
      await page.goto('/numeros');
      const failed = page.waitForEvent('requestfailed', (browserRequest) => browserRequest.method() === 'POST' && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === createPath);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Conectar conta Instagram' }).click();
      await failed;
      expect(durable).toMatchObject({ status: 201 });
      expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(authorizationRequests).toBe(0);
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retomar tentativa de autorização' })).toBeVisible();

      const replay = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === createPath);
      const observed = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${durable!.id}`);
      const authorizationRequest = page.waitForRequest((browserRequest) => new URL(browserRequest.url()).hostname === 'www.instagram.com'
        && new URL(browserRequest.url()).pathname === '/oauth/authorize');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retomar tentativa de autorização' }).click();
      const replayResponse = await replay;
      expect(replayResponse.status()).toBe(201);
      expect(requests).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(requests[1]).toEqual(requests[0]);
      expect(requests[1].key).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i);
      expect(requests[1].body).toEqual({ operation: 'CONNECT' });
      expect((await replayResponse.json()).id).toBe(durable!.id);
      expect((await observed).status()).toBe(200);
      expect(new URL((await authorizationRequest).url()).searchParams.get('state')).toBeTruthy();
      expect(authorizationRequests).toBe(1);
    } finally {
      await page.unroute(`**${createPath}`);
      await page.unroute('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**');
    }
  });

  test('retains the exact creation snapshot after an actual permission-denied observation and clears it only after a terminal read', async ({ page, request, browser }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    const operatorContext = await browser.newContext({ baseURL: FRONTDOOR, ignoreHTTPSErrors: true });
    const operatorPage = await operatorContext.newPage();
    await login(operatorPage, fixture, true);
    const operatorCookie = (await operatorContext.cookies(FRONTDOOR)).map((cookie) => `${cookie.name}=${cookie.value}`).join('; ');
    const createPath = `/api/bff/business-units/${fixture.businessUnitId}/instagram-authorizations`;
    const requests: Array<{ key: string; body: unknown }> = [];
    let durable: { id: string; status: number } | null = null;
    let createCount = 0;
    let deniedReadStatus = 0;
    let deniedReadHash = '';
    let deniedReadBytes = 0;
    let deniedReadDelivered = false;
    let terminalReadStatus = 0;
    let terminalReadState = '';
    let authorizationRequests = 0;
    await page.route('**/api/bff/**instagram-authorizations**', async (route) => {
      const url = new URL(route.request().url());
      if (route.request().method() === 'POST' && url.pathname === createPath) {
        createCount += 1;
        const headers = route.request().headers();
        requests.push({ key: headers['idempotency-key'] ?? '', body: route.request().postDataJSON() });
        const actual = await route.fetch();
        const result = await actual.json() as { id: string };
        durable = { id: result.id, status: actual.status() };
        await route.fulfill({ response: actual });
        return;
      }
      if (route.request().method() === 'GET' && durable && url.pathname === `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${durable.id}` && !deniedReadDelivered) {
        const headers = { ...route.request().headers(), cookie: operatorCookie };
        const actual = await route.fetch({ headers });
        const body = await actual.body();
        deniedReadStatus = actual.status();
        deniedReadBytes = body.byteLength;
        deniedReadHash = createHash('sha256').update(body).digest('hex');
        deniedReadDelivered = true;
        await route.fulfill({ response: actual });
        return;
      }
      if (route.request().method() === 'GET' && durable && url.pathname === `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${durable.id}`) {
        const actual = await route.fetch();
        const body = await actual.json() as { status: string };
        terminalReadStatus = actual.status();
        terminalReadState = body.status;
        await route.fulfill({ response: actual });
        return;
      }
      await route.continue();
    });
    await page.route('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**', async (route) => {
      authorizationRequests += 1;
      await route.abort('blockedbyclient');
    });
    try {
      await page.goto('/numeros');
      const deniedObservation = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname.startsWith('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/'));
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Conectar conta Instagram' }).click();
      const denied = await deniedObservation;
      expect((await denied.finished())).toBeNull();
      expect(denied.status()).toBe(403);
      expect(deniedReadStatus).toBe(403);
      expect(deniedReadBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(deniedReadHash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
      expect(durable?.status).toBe(201);
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retomar tentativa de autorização' })).toBeVisible();
      expect(authorizationRequests).toBe(0);

      const cancelled = await page.context().request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${durable!.id}/cancel`, {
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-requested-with': 'leadshug-web' }, data: {},
      });
      expect(cancelled.status()).toBe(200);
      expect((await cancelled.json()).status).toBe('CANCELLED');

      const replay = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === createPath);
      const terminalRead = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${durable!.id}`);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retomar tentativa de autorização' }).click();
      const replayResponse = await replay;
      expect(replayResponse.status()).toBe(201);
      const terminalResponse = await terminalRead;
      expect(terminalResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      expect((await terminalResponse.finished())).toBeNull();
      expect(terminalReadStatus).toBe(200);
      expect(terminalReadState).toBe('CANCELLED');
      expect(requests).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(requests[1]).toEqual(requests[0]);
      expect((await replayResponse.json()).id).toBe(durable!.id);
      await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'A tentativa anterior foi cancelada.' })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retomar tentativa de autorização' })).toHaveCount(0);
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Conectar conta Instagram' })).toBeEnabled();
      expect(authorizationRequests).toBe(0);
    } finally {
      await operatorContext.close();
      await page.unroute('**/api/bff/**instagram-authorizations**');
      await page.unroute('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**');
    }
  });

  test('paginates fifty-five signed Instagram conversations through the real BFF and rejects a stale list response', async ({ page, request }) => {
    test.setTimeout(150_000);
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await login(page, fixture);
    await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'a');
    for (let index = 0; index < 55; index += 1) {
      await signedIncoming(request, 'a', `mensagem paginada ${index}`, `timeline-ig-page-peer-${String(index).padStart(2, '0')}`);
    }

    let firstListResponse: import('@playwright/test').Response | null = null;
    const firstList = page.waitForResponse((response) => {
      const url = new URL(response.url());
      return response.request().method() === 'GET' && url.pathname === `/api/bff/business-units/${fixture.businessUnitId}/conversations`
        && url.searchParams.get('aba') === 'QUEUED' && url.searchParams.get('pagina') === '1' && url.searchParams.get('limite') === '50';
    });
    await page.goto('/inbox');
    firstListResponse = await firstList;
    expect(firstListResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const firstBody = await firstListResponse.json() as { total: number; pagina: number; temMais: boolean; conversations: Array<{ id: string; display: { accountLabel: string; recipientLabel: string } }> };
    expect(firstBody).toMatchObject({ total: 57, pagina: 1, temMais: true });
    expect(firstBody.conversations).toHaveLength(50);
    expect(firstBody.conversations.every((item) => item.display.accountLabel.includes('timeline_account_a'))).toBe(true);
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item')).toHaveCount(50);

    const secondList = page.waitForResponse((response) => {
      const url = new URL(response.url());
      return response.request().method() === 'GET' && url.pathname === `/api/bff/business-units/${fixture.businessUnitId}/conversations`
        && url.searchParams.get('aba') === 'QUEUED' && url.searchParams.get('pagina') === '2' && url.searchParams.get('limite') === '50';
    });
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Próxima página de conversas' }).click();
    const secondResponse = await secondList;
    expect(secondResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const secondBody = await secondResponse.json() as typeof firstBody;
    expect(secondBody).toMatchObject({ total: 57, pagina: 2, temMais: false });
    expect(secondBody.conversations).toHaveLength(7);
    const secondInstagram = secondBody.conversations.filter((item) => item.display.accountLabel.includes('timeline_account_a'));
    expect(secondInstagram).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(new Set([...firstBody.conversations, ...secondBody.conversations].map((item) => item.id)).size).toBe(57);
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item')).toHaveCount(7);
    const activePeer = secondInstagram[0].display.recipientLabel;
    await page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: activePeer }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha > strong').first()).toHaveText(activePeer);

    let releaseStale: (() => void) | null = null;
    let notifyHeld: (() => void) | null = null;
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { notifyHeld = resolve; });
    const release = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseStale = resolve; });
    const stalePath = `/api/bff/business-units/${fixture.businessUnitId}/conversations`;
    await page.route(`**${stalePath}**`, async (route) => {
      const url = new URL(route.request().url());
      if (url.searchParams.get('aba') !== 'QUEUED' || url.searchParams.get('pagina') !== '2') return route.continue();
      const response = await route.fetch();
      notifyHeld?.();
      await release;
      await route.fulfill({ response });
    });
    try {
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Atualizar lista de conversas' }).click();
      await held;
      const filtered = page.waitForResponse((response) => {
        const url = new URL(response.url());
        return response.request().method() === 'GET' && url.pathname === stalePath
          && url.searchParams.get('aba') === 'IN_SERVICE' && url.searchParams.get('pagina') === '1';
      });
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Em atendimento' }).click();
      const filteredResponse = await filtered;
      expect(filteredResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      expect((await filteredResponse.json()).pagina).toBe(1);
      releaseStale?.();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Página anterior de conversas' })).toBeDisabled();
      await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item')).toHaveCount(0);
      await expect(page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha > strong').first()).toHaveText(activePeer);
    } finally {
      releaseStale?.();
      await page.unroute(`**${stalePath}**`);
    }
  });

  test('proves account isolation, native callback/receipt/reply, held responses, recovery, and operator scope', async ({ page, request, browser }) => {
    test.setTimeout(120_000);
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    const realtimeFrames: RealtimeFrame[] = [];
    let nextSocketId = 0;
    let currentInboxSocketId: number | null = null;
    page.on('websocket', (socket) => {
      const socketId = ++nextSocketId;
      if (isNativeRealtimeSocket(socket.url()) && new URL(page.url()).pathname === '/inbox') currentInboxSocketId = socketId;
      socket.on('framereceived', (frame) => {
        realtimeFrames.push({ socketId, url: socket.url(), payload: typeof frame.payload === 'string' ? frame.payload : frame.payload.toString() });
      });
    });
    await login(page, fixture);
    const attemptA = await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'a');

    await page.goto('/numeros');
    const section = page.locator('section[aria-label="Conexões de canais · Número timeline"]');
    const accountCard = section.locator('article').filter({ hasText: '@timeline_account_a' });
    const waitingHandler = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      await route.fulfill({ status: 200, contentType: 'text/html; charset=utf-8', body: '<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"></head><body><p>Autorização sintética aguardando retorno.</p></body></html>' });
    };
    const reauthorizationRequestPromise = page.waitForRequest((outgoing) => {
      const url = new URL(outgoing.url());
      return url.hostname === 'www.instagram.com' && url.pathname === '/oauth/authorize';
    });
    await page.route('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**', waitingHandler);
    await accountCard.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reautorizar' }).click();
    const reauthorizationRequest = await reauthorizationRequestPromise;
    expect(new URL(reauthorizationRequest.url()).searchParams.get('response_type')).toBe('code');
    await page.waitForURL((url) => url.hostname === 'www.instagram.com' && url.pathname === '/oauth/authorize', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
    await expect(page.getByText('Autorização sintética aguardando retorno.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await page.goBack();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/numeros$/);
    await page.reload();
    await expect(section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar autorização' })).toBeVisible();
    const cancelResponsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && /^\/api\/bff\/instagram-authorizations\/[^/]+\/cancel$/.test(new URL(response.url()).pathname));
    await section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar autorização' }).click();
    const cancelResponse = await cancelResponsePromise;
    expect(cancelResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    expect(await cancelResponse.finished()).toBeNull();
    const attemptsAfterCancel = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/attempts', { buId: fixture.businessUnitId, accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a' })).json();
    expect(attemptsAfterCancel.attempts).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({ operation: 'REAUTHORIZE', status: 'CANCELLED' }));
    await page.unroute('https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize**', waitingHandler);

    await page.addInitScript(() => {
      if (window.name === 'timeline-no-session-storage') Object.defineProperty(window, 'sessionStorage', {
        configurable: false, get: () => { throw new DOMException('synthetic storage denial', 'SecurityError'); },
      });
    });
    await page.evaluate(() => { window.name = 'timeline-no-session-storage'; });
    await connectAccount(page, request, fixture, 'b');
    await page.evaluate(() => { window.name = ''; });
    await assertInstagramFakeCredentialBoundaries(request);

    const initialProviderState = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    const existingEffects = initialProviderState.effects.length;

    currentInboxSocketId = null;
    await page.goto('/inbox');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    await expect.poll(() => hasRealtimeConnection(realtimeFrames, currentInboxSocketId, fixture.businessUnitId)).toBe(true);
    const incomingA = await signedIncoming(request, 'a', 'entrada atual da conta');
    await expect.poll(() => countRealtimeIncoming(realtimeFrames, currentInboxSocketId, incomingA.conversationId!, fixture.businessUnitId)).toBe(1);
    const accountAThread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: '@timeline_account_a' });
    await expect(accountAThread).toBeVisible();
    await expect(accountAThread).toContainText('Instagram @timeline_account_a');
    const incomingB = await signedIncoming(request, 'b', 'entrada atual da conta');
    await expect.poll(() => countRealtimeIncoming(realtimeFrames, currentInboxSocketId, incomingB.conversationId!, fixture.businessUnitId)).toBe(1);
