    const accountBThread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: '@timeline_account_b' });
    await expect(accountBThread).toBeVisible();
    await expect(accountBThread).toContainText('Instagram @timeline_account_b');
    expect(incomingA.conversationId).not.toBe(incomingB.conversationId);
    const observedA = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/observe', { accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a' })).json();
    const observedB = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/observe', { accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-b' })).json();
    expect(observedA).toMatchObject({ connected: true, routes: 1, qualifiedInbound: 1 });
    expect(observedA.receiptVerifiedAt).toBeTruthy();
    expect(observedB).toMatchObject({ connected: true, routes: 1, qualifiedInbound: 1 });
    expect(observedB.receiptVerifiedAt).toBeTruthy();

    const aThread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: '@timeline_account_a' });
    await aThread.click();
    await expect(page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha').getByText(/@timeline_account_a/)).toBeVisible();
    const firstComposer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await firstComposer.fill('resposta Instagram A');
    const firstResponsePath = `/api/bff/conversations/${incomingA.conversationId}/messages`;
    const firstResponsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === firstResponsePath);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    const firstResponse = await firstResponsePromise;
    expect(firstResponse.status()).toBe(201);
    await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: 'resposta Instagram A' })).toBeVisible();

    const effectsAfterA = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    expect(effectsAfterA.effects).toContainEqual({ operation: 'message.send', accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', peerId: 'timeline-ig-peer' });

    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await composer.fill('rascunho novo da conta A');
    const preparedDraftResponse = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${incomingA.conversationId}/messages`);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    expect((await preparedDraftResponse).status()).toBe(201);
    await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: 'rascunho novo da conta A' })).toBeVisible();

    const acceptedHeldPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${incomingA.conversationId}/messages`;
    await hold(request, incomingA.conversationId!);
    let acceptedReleased = false;
    try {
      const acceptedHeldResponsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === acceptedHeldPath);
      await composer.fill('resposta Instagram A aceita retida');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
      const acceptedPending = await waitForNativeHold(request);
      expect(acceptedPending.status).toBe(201);
      const committedAcceptedEffects = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();

      await composer.fill('rascunho após ACK aceito');

      expect(acceptedRelease.status()).toBe(200);
      acceptedReleased = true;

      const acceptedBody = await acceptedHeldResponse.body();
      const acceptedCompletion = await (await request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/state`)).json();
      expect(acceptedCompletion.completed).toMatchObject({ status: 201, bodyBytes: acceptedBody.byteLength,
        bodyHash: createHash('sha256').update(acceptedBody).digest('hex') });
      expect(acceptedCompletion.completed.bodyHash).toBe(acceptedPending.bodyHash);
      expect(acceptedHeldResponse.headers()['content-type']).toBe(acceptedPending.headers['content-type']);
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue('rascunho após ACK aceito');

    } finally {

      { operation: 'message.send', accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', peerId: 'timeline-ig-peer' },

      { operation: 'message.send', accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', peerId: 'timeline-ig-peer' },

      { operation: 'message.send', accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-b', peerId: 'timeline-ig-peer' },
    ]);
    const beforeBad = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/observe', { accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a' })).json();
    const wrongSignature = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/incoming', { accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', text: 'rejeitada', signatureMode: 'wrong' });
    expect((await wrongSignature.json()).status).not.toBe(200);
    const crossTarget = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/incoming', {
      accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', recipientAccountId: 'timeline-ig-account-b', text: 'destinatário de outra conta',
    });
    const crossTargetResult = await crossTarget.json() as Incoming;
    expect(crossTargetResult.status).toBe(200);
    expect(crossTargetResult.conversationId).toBeNull();
    expect(crossTargetResult.messageId).toBeNull();
    const afterBad = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/observe', { accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a' })).json();
    expect(afterBad.qualifiedInbound).toBe(beforeBad.qualifiedInbound);

    const staleResponse = await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/incoming', {
      accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a', peerId: 'timeline-ig-expired-peer', timestampMode: 'stale',
      text: 'entrada antiga fora da janela',
    });
    const stale = await staleResponse.json() as Incoming;
    expect(stale.status).toBe(200);
    expect(stale.conversationId).toBeTruthy();
    expect(stale.messageId).toBeTruthy();
    expect(stale.timestamp).toBeNull();
    const afterStale = await (await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/observe', { accountId: 'timeline-ig-account-a' })).json();
    expect(afterStale).toMatchObject({ routes: 2, qualifiedInbound: beforeBad.qualifiedInbound });
    expect(afterStale.receiptVerifiedAt).toBeTruthy();
    expect(new Date(afterStale.receiptVerifiedAt).getTime()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(new Date(beforeBad.receiptVerifiedAt).getTime());

    currentInboxSocketId = null;
    await page.goto('/inbox');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    const expiredThread = page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: 'fixture_expired_peer' });
    await expect(expiredThread).toBeVisible();
    await expiredThread.click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Fora da janela de 24h do canal selecionado' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true })).toBeDisabled();

    await page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').filter({ hasText: '@timeline_account_b' }).filter({ hasText: 'fixture_peer' }).click();
    const unknownComposer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await expect(unknownComposer).toBeVisible();
    await fixturePost(request, '/provider/instagram/config', { sendMode: 'unknown' });
    await unknownComposer.fill('rascunho Instagram sem recibo');
    const path = `/api/bff/conversations/${incomingB.conversationId}/messages`;
    const effectsBeforeUnknown = (await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json()).effects.filter((item: { operation: string }) => item.operation === 'message.send').length;
    const failedResponse = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === path);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    expect((await failedResponse).status()).toBe(409);
    await expect(unknownComposer).toHaveValue('rascunho Instagram sem recibo');
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toBeVisible();
    const stateBeforeRefresh = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    expect(stateBeforeRefresh.effects.filter((item: { operation: string }) => item.operation === 'message.send')).toHaveLength(effectsBeforeUnknown + 1);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Atualizar opções' }).click();
    await expect(unknownComposer).toHaveValue('rascunho Instagram sem recibo');
    const stateAfterRefresh = await (await fixtureGet(request, '/provider/instagram/state')).json();
    expect(stateAfterRefresh.effects.filter((item: { operation: string }) => item.operation === 'message.send')).toHaveLength(effectsBeforeUnknown + 1);

    const operatorContext = await browser.newContext({ ignoreHTTPSErrors: true });
    try {
      const operatorPage = await operatorContext.newPage();
      await login(operatorPage, fixture, true);
      await operatorPage.goto(`${FRONTDOOR}/numeros`);
      const operatorSection = operatorPage.locator('section[aria-label="Conexões de canais · Número timeline"]');
      await expect(operatorSection.getByRole('button', { name: 'Conectar conta Instagram' })).toHaveCount(0);
      await expect(operatorSection.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reautorizar' })).toHaveCount(0);
      const isolated = await operatorContext.request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${attemptA.id}`);
      expect([403, 404]).toContain(isolated.status());
      expect(await isolated.text()).not.toMatch(/access_token|refresh_token|authorization_code|ciphertext/i);
    } finally { await operatorContext.close(); }
  });
});


## FULL SOURCE BODY web-app/src/componentes/NovaConversa.tsx

import { FormEvent, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { api, ErroDaApi, templateApi, type TemplateSummary } from '../api';

interface OpenedConversation { conversaId: string; criada: boolean; contact: string }
const templateKey = (summary: TemplateSummary) => `${summary.remoteTemplateId}:${summary.language ?? ''}`;

export interface StartOptions {
  conversationId: string;
  initialRecipientPlatform: 'WHATSAPP' | null;
  channels: Array<{
    id: string;
    type: string;
    generation: number;
    pairingEpoch?: number;
    activeCredentialVersion?: number;
    supportedKinds: string[];
    availability: { text: string | null; 'approved-template': string | null };
  }>;
  activeAttempt: InitialAttempt | null;
  ordinaryAdmissions: Array<{ id: string; state: string; expiresAt: string; dispatchDeadlineAt: string | null; createdAt: string }>;
  admissionsTruncated: boolean;
  recentAttempts: InitialAttempt[];
  attemptsTruncated: boolean;
}

export interface StartOptionsReadTicket {
  ticket: number;
  actorId: string;
  buId: string;
  conversationId: string;
  generation: number;
  navigation: number;
}

export interface InitialAttempt {
  id: string;
  state: string;
  externalAccepted?: boolean;
  providerAcceptance: string;
  receiptPersistence: string;
  localMessageState: string;
  recoveryAction: 'RECONCILE' | 'ACK_AFTER_DEADLINE' | null;
  eligibleAt?: string | null;
  slotReleasedAt?: string | null;
  riskAcknowledgementAvailable?: boolean;
}

export interface RecoveredAttemptFact {
  attempt: InitialAttempt;
  basedOnOptions: StartOptions;
}

/** A local recovery observation expires as soon as the parent accepts another options object. */
export function recoveredAttemptForOptions(fact: RecoveredAttemptFact | null, options: StartOptions): InitialAttempt | null {
  return fact?.basedOnOptions === options ? fact.attempt : null;
}

function canonical(value: unknown): string {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonical).join(',')}]`;
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    const row = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    return `{${Object.keys(row).sort().map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical(row[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
  }
  return JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';
}

/** Computes only the server-defined canonical semantic hash; it does not parse or authorize template grammar. */
export async function fingerprintInitialTemplate(summary: TemplateSummary): Promise<string> {
  const components = summary.components as Array<{ type?: unknown }>;
  const semanticComponents = [
    ...components.filter((component) => component.type === 'HEADER'),
    ...components.filter((component) => component.type === 'BODY'),
    ...components.filter((component) => component.type === 'FOOTER'),
  ];
  const canonicalSource = canonical({ version: 'initial-template-text-v1', remoteTemplateId: summary.remoteTemplateId,
    name: summary.name, language: summary.language, category: summary.category, parameterFormat: summary.parameterFormat,
    components: semanticComponents });
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalSource));
  return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

function templateParameterNames(summary: TemplateSummary): string[] {
  const body = (summary.components as Array<{ type?: unknown; parameters?: unknown }>).find((component) => component.type === 'BODY');
  const parameters = Array.isArray(body?.parameters) ? body.parameters.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === 'string') : [];
  const unique = [...new Set(parameters)];
  return summary.parameterFormat === 'POSITIONAL' ? unique.sort((left, right) => Number(left) - Number(right)) : unique;
}

function attemptMessage(attempt: InitialAttempt): string {
  if (attempt.externalAccepted && attempt.localMessageState !== 'RECORDED') {
    return 'Envio aceito pelo canal; registro da conversa pendente. Aceite não confirma entrega.';
  }
  if (attempt.state === 'AMBIGUOUS' || attempt.providerAcceptance === 'UNKNOWN') {
    return 'Resultado do envio ainda desconhecido. Consulte o estado antes de qualquer nova ação.';
  }
  return 'O primeiro envio ainda está sendo processado.';
}

function operationError(cause: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.codigo === 'ACK_TOO_EARLY') {
    return cause.eligibleAt
      ? `A confirmação de risco só estará disponível após ${new Date(cause.eligibleAt).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}.`
      : 'A confirmação de risco ainda não está disponível.';
  }
  return fallback;
}

function availabilityMessage(code: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  switch (code) {
    case 'JANELA_FECHADA': return 'A janela de texto livre foi encerrada. Escolha um modelo aprovado para iniciar o contato.';
    case 'UNSUPPORTED_KIND': return 'Este canal não oferece esse tipo de primeira mensagem.';
    case 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY': return 'O canal ainda não está pronto para iniciar o contato.';
    case 'CHANNEL_IDENTITY_NOT_VERIFIED': return 'A identidade do canal precisa ser conferida antes do envio.';
    case 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL': return 'A credencial do canal precisa ser atualizada.';
    default: return code ? 'Este tipo de envio está indisponível para o canal selecionado.' : null;
  }
}

function channelLabel(type: string): string {
  if (type === 'OFICIAL') return 'WhatsApp oficial';
  if (type === 'NAO_OFICIAL') return 'WhatsApp não oficial';
  return 'Canal de mensagens';
}

interface Preview {
  text: string;
  previewFingerprint: string;
  channel: { channelId: string; channelOrigin: string; generation: number; requiresWindow: boolean };
}

interface InitialSendSnapshot {
  key: string;
  request: string;
  body: { channelId: string; expectedGeneration: number; mensagem: Record<string, unknown>; previewFingerprint: string };
  attemptId?: string;
  hadUnknownOutcome?: boolean;
}

// Both text and rendered templates are capped at 4,096 code points. In the
// worst case JSON escapes each control character to six ASCII characters.
const MAX_INITIAL_SNAPSHOT_REQUEST_LENGTH = 32_768;

export function isInitialSendSnapshot(value: unknown): value is InitialSendSnapshot {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
  const snapshot = value as Partial<InitialSendSnapshot>;
  const body = snapshot.body;
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(snapshot.key ?? '')
    || typeof snapshot.request !== 'string' || snapshot.request.length > MAX_INITIAL_SNAPSHOT_REQUEST_LENGTH
    || !body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)
    || !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(body.channelId)
    || !Number.isInteger(body.expectedGeneration) || body.expectedGeneration < 1
    || typeof body.previewFingerprint !== 'string' || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/i.test(body.previewFingerprint)
    || Object.keys(body).sort().join(',') !== 'channelId,expectedGeneration,mensagem,previewFingerprint'
    || !body.mensagem || typeof body.mensagem !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body.mensagem)
    || canonical(body) !== snapshot.request) return false;
  const message = body.mensagem;
  if (message.tipo === 'TEXTO') {
    return Object.keys(message).sort().join(',') === 'texto,tipo'
      && typeof message.texto === 'string' && message.texto.length > 0 && [...message.texto].length <= 4096;
  }
  if (message.tipo !== 'TEMPLATE' || typeof message.templateId !== 'string' || message.templateId.length === 0 || message.templateId.length > 200
    || typeof message.language !== 'string' || message.language.length === 0 || message.language.length > 64
    || typeof message.expectedDefinitionFingerprint !== 'string' || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/i.test(message.expectedDefinitionFingerprint)
    || (message.catalogAfter !== undefined && (typeof message.catalogAfter !== 'string' || message.catalogAfter.length > 512))
    || !(Array.isArray(message.parameters) || (message.parameters && typeof message.parameters === 'object'))
    || (Array.isArray(message.parameters) && (message.parameters.length > 20 || message.parameters.some((item) => typeof item !== 'string' || [...item].length > 1024)))
    || (message.parameters && !Array.isArray(message.parameters) && (Object.keys(message.parameters).length > 20
      || Object.entries(message.parameters).some(([name, item]) => name.length > 40 || typeof item !== 'string' || [...item].length > 1024)))) return false;
  return Object.keys(message).every((key) => ['tipo', 'templateId', 'language', 'catalogAfter', 'expectedDefinitionFingerprint', 'parameters'].includes(key));
}

export function NovaConversa({ buId, onOpened, onClose, onAlert, onClearAlert }: {
  buId: string;
  onOpened: (result: OpenedConversation) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAlert: (source: string, message: string) => number;
  onClearAlert: (source: string, version: number) => void;
}) {
  const [telefone, setTelefone] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const openGeneration = useRef(0);
  const openBu = useRef(buId);
  const openLatch = useRef(false);
  const openMounted = useRef(true);
  const alertVersion = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const alertSource = `open-conversation:${buId}`;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (error) alertVersion.current = onAlert(alertSource, error);
    else if (alertVersion.current !== null) { onClearAlert(alertSource, alertVersion.current); alertVersion.current = null; }
  }, [error, alertSource, onAlert, onClearAlert]);
  useEffect(() => () => { if (alertVersion.current !== null) onClearAlert(alertSource, alertVersion.current); }, [alertSource, onClearAlert]);
  useEffect(() => {
    openMounted.current = true;
    return () => { openMounted.current = false; openGeneration.current += 1; };
  }, []);
  if (openBu.current !== buId) {
    openBu.current = buId;
    openGeneration.current += 1;
  }

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!buId || openLatch.current) return;
    openLatch.current = true;
    const requestGeneration = openGeneration.current;
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const result = await api.post<OpenedConversation>(`/business-units/${buId}/conversations/open`, { telefone });
      if (!openMounted.current || requestGeneration !== openGeneration.current || openBu.current !== buId) return;
      onOpened(result);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (openMounted.current && requestGeneration === openGeneration.current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível abrir a conversa.');
    } finally {
      if (openMounted.current && requestGeneration === openGeneration.current) setSaving(false);
      openLatch.current = false;
    }
  }

  return <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="nova-conversa-titulo" className="cartao grade">
    <div className="linha"><strong id="nova-conversa-titulo">Nova conversa</strong><button type="button" onClick={onClose} disabled={saving}>Fechar</button></div>
    <form className="grade" onSubmit={(event) => void submit(event)}>
      <label>Telefone com DDI explícito
        <input autoFocus inputMode="tel" autoComplete="tel" maxLength={40} value={telefone} onChange={(event) => setTelefone(event.target.value)} />
      </label>
      <span className="suave">Informe o código do país. O sistema não adivinha o DDI.</span>
      <button className="primario" disabled={!telefone.trim() || saving}>{saving ? 'Abrindo…' : 'Abrir ou reutilizar'}</button>
    </form>
  </div>;
}

export function PrimeiraMensagem({ actorId, buId, conversationId, options, onOptions, beginOptionsRead, isOptionsReadCurrent, onComplete, onCancel, onAlert, onClearAlert }: {
  actorId: string;
  buId: string;
  conversationId: string;
  options: StartOptions;
  onOptions: (options: StartOptions, ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => void;
  beginOptionsRead: () => StartOptionsReadTicket | null;
  isOptionsReadCurrent: (ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => boolean;
  onComplete: () => void;
  onCancel?: () => void;
  onAlert: (source: string, message: string) => number;
  onClearAlert: (source: string, version: number) => void;
}) {
  const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState('');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<'text' | 'approved-template'>('text');
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<TemplateSummary[]>([]);
  const [catalogAfter, setCatalogAfter] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [catalogCursorByTemplate, setCatalogCursorByTemplate] = useState<Record<string, string | null>>({});
  const [selectedTemplate, setSelectedTemplate] = useState('');
  const [parameterValues, setParameterValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<Preview | null>(null);
  const [previewing, setPreviewing] = useState(false);
  const [sendingOwner, setSendingOwner] = useState<{ id: number; ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket } | null>(null);
  const [catalogBusy, setCatalogBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [attemptError, setAttemptErrorState] = useState<{ attemptId: string; message: string } | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [noticeAttemptId, setNoticeAttemptId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [confirmedRiskAttemptId, setConfirmedRiskAttemptId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [retryOnly, setRetryOnly] = useState(false);
  const [sendSnapshot, setSendSnapshot] = useState<InitialSendSnapshot | null>(null);
  const [snapshotLoaded, setSnapshotLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [storageReadUnavailable, setStorageReadUnavailable] = useState(false);
  const [recoveredAttempt, setRecoveredAttempt] = useState<RecoveredAttemptFact | null>(null);
  const generation = useRef(0);
  const actionSequence = useRef(0);
  const previewLatch = useRef(false);
  const sendLatch = useRef(false);
  const sendSnapshotRef = useRef<InitialSendSnapshot | null>(null);
  const mounted = useRef(true);
  const alertVersion = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const alertSource = `initial-message:${actorId}:${buId}:${conversationId}`;
  const catalogRequest = useRef(0);
  const storageKey = `leadshug:initial-send:v1:${encodeURIComponent(actorId)}:${encodeURIComponent(buId)}:${encodeURIComponent(conversationId)}`;

  const saveSnapshot = useCallback((value: InitialSendSnapshot | null): boolean => {
    sendSnapshotRef.current = value;
    setSendSnapshot(value);
    try {
      if (value) sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(value));
      else sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
      return true;
    } catch { return false; }
  }, [storageKey]);

  const clearSnapshot = useCallback(() => {
    saveSnapshot(null);
    setRetryOnly(false);
  }, [saveSnapshot]);

  const ownsSnapshot = (captured: InitialSendSnapshot, currentGeneration: number) => {
    const currentSnapshot = sendSnapshotRef.current;
    if (!currentSnapshot) return false;
    return mounted.current && generation.current === currentGeneration
      && currentSnapshot.key === captured.key && currentSnapshot.request === captured.request
      && canonical(currentSnapshot.body) === captured.request && canonical(captured.body) === captured.request;
  };

  const restoreSnapshot = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey);
      const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) as unknown : null;
      if (isInitialSendSnapshot(parsed)) {
        const restored = { ...parsed, hadUnknownOutcome: true };
        sendSnapshotRef.current = restored;
        setSendSnapshot(restored);
        setRetryOnly(true);
      } else if (raw) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
      }
      setStorageReadUnavailable(false);
      setError(null);
    } catch {
      // A failed read cannot prove that no idempotency snapshot exists.
      setStorageReadUnavailable(true);
    }
  }, [storageKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    generation.current += 1;
    return () => { generation.current += 1; };
  }, [conversationId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => { mounted.current = false; };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSnapshotLoaded(false);
    sendSnapshotRef.current = null;
    setSendSnapshot(null);
    setRetryOnly(false);
    setStorageReadUnavailable(false);
    restoreSnapshot();
    setSnapshotLoaded(true);
  }, [storageKey, restoreSnapshot]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const snapshot = sendSnapshotRef.current;
    if (!snapshot?.attemptId) return;
    const durable = [options.activeAttempt, ...options.recentAttempts].find((item) => item?.id === snapshot.attemptId);
    if (durable && (durable.state === 'SUCCEEDED' || durable.state === 'REJECTED' || durable.slotReleasedAt)) clearSnapshot();
  }, [options.activeAttempt, options.recentAttempts, clearSnapshot]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (recoveredAttempt && recoveredAttempt.basedOnOptions !== options) setRecoveredAttempt(null);
  }, [options, recoveredAttempt]);

  const channel = options.channels.find((item) => item.id === channelId) ?? null;
  const selectedSummary = catalog.find((item) => templateKey(item) === selectedTemplate) ?? null;
  const parameterNames = selectedSummary ? templateParameterNames(selectedSummary) : [];
  const kindAvailability = channel?.availability[kind];
  const kindUnavailable = !channel || kindAvailability !== null;
  const kindUnavailableMessage = availabilityMessage(kindAvailability);
  const storageBlocked = !snapshotLoaded || storageReadUnavailable;
  const sending = sendingOwner !== null && isOptionsReadCurrent(sendingOwner.ticket);
  const currentRecoveredAttempt = recoveredAttemptForOptions(recoveredAttempt, options);
  const attempt = options.activeAttempt ?? currentRecoveredAttempt ?? options.recentAttempts.find((item) => item.slotReleasedAt === null && item.recoveryAction !== null) ?? null;
  const ordinaryUncertain = options.ordinaryAdmissions.some((item) => ['SENDING', 'UNCERTAIN'].includes(item.state));
  const locked = Boolean(options.activeAttempt || (currentRecoveredAttempt && currentRecoveredAttempt.slotReleasedAt === null)) || ordinaryUncertain;
  const visibleNotice = noticeAttemptId === null || noticeAttemptId === attempt?.id ? notice : null;
  const visibleAttemptError = attemptError !== null && attemptError.attemptId === attempt?.id ? attemptError.message : null;
  const alertMessage = storageReadUnavailable ? 'Não foi possível verificar a tentativa salva neste navegador.' : error ?? visibleAttemptError;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (alertMessage) alertVersion.current = onAlert(alertSource, alertMessage);
    else if (alertVersion.current !== null) { onClearAlert(alertSource, alertVersion.current); alertVersion.current = null; }
  }, [alertMessage, alertSource, onAlert, onClearAlert]);
  useEffect(() => () => { if (alertVersion.current !== null) onClearAlert(alertSource, alertVersion.current); }, [alertSource, onClearAlert]);
  const currentGeneration = () => generation.current;
  const beginAction = () => {
    const ticket = beginOptionsRead();
    if (!ticket || !mounted.current || !isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) return null;
    return { ticket, owner: ++actionSequence.current };
  };
  const actionIsCurrent = (owner: number, ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket, current: number) =>
    mounted.current && generation.current === current && actionSequence.current === owner && isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket);
  const setActionTicket = (owner: number, ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => {
    if (actionSequence.current === owner) setSendingOwner({ id: owner, ticket });
  };
  const rememberAttempt = (attemptFact: InitialAttempt, basedOnOptions: StartOptions) => {
    setRecoveredAttempt({ attempt: attemptFact, basedOnOptions });
  };
  const publishAttemptError = (attemptId: string, message: string) => setAttemptErrorState({ attemptId, message });
  const clearAttemptError = (attemptId?: string) => setAttemptErrorState((current) => !attemptId || current?.attemptId === attemptId ? null : current);
  const publishNotice = (message: string | null) => { setNotice(message); setNoticeAttemptId(null); };
  const publishAttemptNotice = (attemptId: string, message: string) => { setNotice(message); setNoticeAttemptId(attemptId); };
  const invalidateDraft = () => { generation.current += 1; setPreview(null); setError(null); };

  useEffect(() => {
    generation.current += 1;
    setPreview(null);
    setError(null);
    setCatalogCursorByTemplate({});
    if (kind !== 'approved-template' || !channelId) return;
    const owner = ++catalogRequest.current;
    let current = true;
    setCatalog([]);
    setCatalogAfter(null);
    setSelectedTemplate('');
    setParameterValues({});
    setCatalogBusy(true);
    templateApi.catalog(buId, channelId).then((page) => {
      if (!current) return;
      setCatalog(page.items ?? []);
      setCatalogAfter(page.nextCursor);
      setCatalogCursorByTemplate(Object.fromEntries((page.items ?? []).map((item) => [templateKey(item), null])));
      publishNotice(page.available ? null : 'Atualize o catálogo para observar os modelos desta conta.');
    }).catch((cause) => { if (current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível ler o catálogo.'); })
      .finally(() => { if (mounted.current && catalogRequest.current === owner) setCatalogBusy(false); });
    return () => { current = false; if (catalogRequest.current === owner) catalogRequest.current += 1; };
  }, [buId, channelId, kind]);

  useEffect(() => {
    generation.current += 1;
    setPreview(null);
  }, [channel?.generation, channel?.pairingEpoch, channel?.activeCredentialVersion]);

  function messageFor(kindValue = kind, content = text): Record<string, unknown> {
    if (kindValue === 'text') return { tipo: 'TEXTO', texto: content };
    const summary = selectedSummary;
    if (!summary || !summary.language) throw new Error('Selecione um modelo observado no catálogo.');
    const values = summary.parameterFormat === 'POSITIONAL'
      ? parameterNames.map((name) => parameterValues[name] ?? '')
      : Object.fromEntries(parameterNames.map((name) => [name, parameterValues[name] ?? '']));
    return { tipo: 'TEMPLATE', templateId: summary.remoteTemplateId, language: summary.language,
      ...(catalogCursorByTemplate[selectedTemplate] ? { catalogAfter: catalogCursorByTemplate[selectedTemplate] } : {}),
      expectedDefinitionFingerprint: '0'.repeat(64), parameters: values };
  }

  async function withTemplateFingerprint(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    const message = messageFor();
    if (kind === 'text') return message;
    const summary = selectedSummary;
    if (!summary) return message;
    return { ...message, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: await fingerprintInitialTemplate(summary) };
  }

  async function refreshCatalog() {
    if (!channel || catalogBusy) return;
    invalidateDraft();
    const owner = ++catalogRequest.current;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    setCatalogBusy(true); setError(null); publishNotice(null);
    try {
      const result = await templateApi.refreshCatalog(buId, channel.id);
      if (generation.current !== current || catalogRequest.current !== owner) return;
      if ('phase' in result) { publishNotice('Atualização do catálogo em andamento; tente novamente em instantes.'); return; }
      setCatalog(result.items ?? []); setCatalogAfter(result.nextCursor);
      setCatalogCursorByTemplate(Object.fromEntries((result.items ?? []).map((item) => [templateKey(item), null])));
      setSelectedTemplate('');
      publishNotice(result.available ? 'Catálogo atualizado.' : 'O catálogo não está disponível para este canal.');
    } catch (cause) { if (generation.current === current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível atualizar o catálogo.'); }
    finally { if (mounted.current && catalogRequest.current === owner) setCatalogBusy(false); }
  }

  async function loadMoreTemplates() {
    if (!channel || !catalogAfter || catalogBusy) return;
    invalidateDraft();
    const owner = ++catalogRequest.current;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    setCatalogBusy(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const page = await templateApi.catalog(buId, channel.id, catalogAfter);
      if (generation.current !== current || catalogRequest.current !== owner) return;
      setCatalog((previous) => [...previous, ...page.items]); setCatalogAfter(page.nextCursor);
      setCatalogCursorByTemplate((previous) => ({ ...previous, ...Object.fromEntries(page.items.map((item) => [templateKey(item), catalogAfter])) }));
    } catch (cause) { if (generation.current === current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível carregar mais modelos.'); }
    finally { if (mounted.current && catalogRequest.current === owner) setCatalogBusy(false); }
  }

  async function previewMessage() {
    if (previewLatch.current || !snapshotLoaded || storageReadUnavailable || !channel || locked || retryOnly) return;
    previewLatch.current = true;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    setPreviewing(true); setError(null); publishNotice(null); setPreview(null);
    try {
      const message = await withTemplateFingerprint();
      if (!mounted.current || generation.current !== current) return;
      const result = await api.post<Preview>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/initial-message-preview`, {
        channelId: channel.id, expectedGeneration: channel.generation, mensagem: message,
      });
      if (!mounted.current || generation.current !== current) return;
      setPreview(result);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (generation.current === current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : 'A prévia não está disponível.');
    } finally {
      previewLatch.current = false;
      if (mounted.current) setPreviewing(false);
    }
  }

  async function resumeOriginal() {
    const snapshot = sendSnapshotRef.current;
    if (!snapshot || !snapshotLoaded || storageReadUnavailable || sendLatch.current) return;
    const optionsAtStart = options;
    const action = beginAction();
    if (!action) return;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    sendLatch.current = true;
    let ownedTicket = action.ticket;
    setSendingOwner({ id: action.owner, ticket: ownedTicket }); setError(null); publishNotice(null);
    try {
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, action.ticket, current)) return;
      const result = await api.postWithHeaders<{ attempt: InitialAttempt }>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/initial-messages`, snapshot.body, {
        'Idempotency-Key': snapshot.key,
      });
      if (ownsSnapshot(snapshot, current)) {
        saveSnapshot({ ...snapshot, attemptId: result.attempt.id, hadUnknownOutcome: true });
        if (result.attempt.state === 'SUCCEEDED' || result.attempt.state === 'REJECTED' || result.attempt.slotReleasedAt) clearSnapshot();
      }
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, action.ticket, current)) return;
      rememberAttempt(result.attempt, optionsAtStart);
      const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
      if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
      publishAttemptNotice(result.attempt.id, result.attempt.state === 'SUCCEEDED' ? 'Primeira mensagem registrada na conversa.' : 'Envio em andamento; consulte o estado antes de tentar novamente.');
      if (result.attempt.state === 'SUCCEEDED') onComplete();
    } catch (cause) {
      if (ownsSnapshot(snapshot, current)) {
        if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.attemptId && cause.state) {
          saveSnapshot({ ...snapshot, attemptId: cause.attemptId, hadUnknownOutcome: true });
        } else if (!(cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.preAdmission === true)) {
          saveSnapshot({ ...snapshot, hadUnknownOutcome: true });
        }
        if (!(cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.preAdmission === true)) setRetryOnly(true);
      }
      if (actionIsCurrent(action.owner, action.ticket, current)) {
        const message = operationError(cause, 'O resultado ainda não foi confirmado. Consulte a tentativa original antes de enviar outra mensagem.');
        if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.attemptId) publishAttemptError(cause.attemptId, message);
        else setError(message);
        const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.attemptId : undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
        if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
      }
    } finally {
      if (actionSequence.current === action.owner) {
        sendLatch.current = false;
        if (mounted.current) setSendingOwner((value) => value?.id === action.owner && value.ticket === ownedTicket ? null : value);
      }
    }
  }

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (retryOnly && sendSnapshotRef.current?.hadUnknownOutcome) { await resumeOriginal(); return; }
    if (sendLatch.current || !snapshotLoaded || storageReadUnavailable || !channel || !preview || locked || retryOnly) return;
    const optionsAtStart = options;
    const action = beginAction();
    if (!action) return;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    sendLatch.current = true;
    let ownedTicket = action.ticket;
    let capturedSnapshot: InitialSendSnapshot | null = null;
    setSendingOwner({ id: action.owner, ticket: ownedTicket }); setError(null); publishNotice(null);
    try {
      const mensagem = await withTemplateFingerprint();
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, action.ticket, current)) return;
      const body = { channelId: channel.id, expectedGeneration: channel.generation, mensagem, previewFingerprint: preview.previewFingerprint };
      const request = canonical(body);
      let snapshot = sendSnapshotRef.current;
      if (snapshot && snapshot.request !== request) throw new Error('O envio anterior não foi confirmado. Consulte o estado antes de alterar ou repetir a mensagem.');
      if (!snapshot) {
        snapshot = { key: crypto.randomUUID(), request, body, hadUnknownOutcome: false };
        if (!saveSnapshot(snapshot)) {
          sendSnapshotRef.current = null;
          setSendSnapshot(null);
          setError('Não foi possível guardar a tentativa neste navegador. Nenhuma mensagem foi enviada.');
          return;
        }
      }
      capturedSnapshot = snapshot;
      const result = await api.postWithHeaders<{ attempt: InitialAttempt }>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/initial-messages`, snapshot.body,
        { 'Idempotency-Key': snapshot.key });
      if (ownsSnapshot(snapshot, current)) {
        saveSnapshot({ ...snapshot, attemptId: result.attempt.id });
        if (result.attempt.state === 'SUCCEEDED' || result.attempt.state === 'REJECTED' || result.attempt.slotReleasedAt) {
          clearSnapshot();
        } else {
          setRetryOnly(true);
        }
      }
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, action.ticket, current)) return;
      rememberAttempt(result.attempt, optionsAtStart);
      const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
      if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
      publishAttemptNotice(result.attempt.id, result.attempt.state === 'SUCCEEDED' ? 'Primeira mensagem registrada na conversa.' : 'Envio em andamento; consulte o estado antes de tentar novamente.');
      if (result.attempt.state === 'SUCCEEDED') onComplete();
    } catch (cause) {
      const conclusivelyPreAdmission = cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.preAdmission === true
        && !retryOnly && !sendSnapshotRef.current?.hadUnknownOutcome;
      if (capturedSnapshot && ownsSnapshot(capturedSnapshot, current) && !conclusivelyPreAdmission) {
        saveSnapshot({ ...capturedSnapshot, hadUnknownOutcome: true });
        setRetryOnly(true);
      }
      if (actionIsCurrent(action.owner, action.ticket, current)) {
        if (conclusivelyPreAdmission && capturedSnapshot && ownsSnapshot(capturedSnapshot, current)) {
          clearSnapshot();
          setPreview(null);
          setError('A prévia precisa ser atualizada antes de iniciar. Revise as opções e gere uma nova prévia.');
          const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
          if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
          return;
        }
        if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.attemptId && cause.state && capturedSnapshot && ownsSnapshot(capturedSnapshot, current)) {
          const fallback: InitialAttempt = {
            id: cause.attemptId, state: cause.state, externalAccepted: cause.providerAcceptance === 'CONFIRMED',
            providerAcceptance: cause.providerAcceptance ?? 'UNKNOWN', receiptPersistence: cause.receiptPersistence ?? 'UNCONFIRMED',
            localMessageState: cause.localMessageState ?? 'UNCONFIRMED', eligibleAt: cause.eligibleAt ?? null, slotReleasedAt: null,
            recoveryAction: cause.state === 'AMBIGUOUS' ? 'ACK_AFTER_DEADLINE' : ['SENDING', 'EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED', 'EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED_LOCAL_BLOCKED'].includes(cause.state) ? 'RECONCILE' : null,
            riskAcknowledgementAvailable: ['AMBIGUOUS', 'EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED_LOCAL_BLOCKED'].includes(cause.state),
          };
          rememberAttempt(fallback, optionsAtStart);
          saveSnapshot({ ...capturedSnapshot, attemptId: cause.attemptId, hadUnknownOutcome: true });
        }
        const message = operationError(cause, 'Não foi possível confirmar o resultado. Consulte o estado antes de tentar novamente.');
        if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.attemptId) publishAttemptError(cause.attemptId, message);
        else setError(message);
        const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.attemptId : undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
        if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
      }
    } finally {
      if (actionSequence.current === action.owner) {
        sendLatch.current = false;
        if (mounted.current) setSendingOwner((value) => value?.id === action.owner && value.ticket === ownedTicket ? null : value);
      }
    }
  }

  async function reloadOptions(expectedAttemptId?: string, onTicket?: (ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => void): Promise<StartOptionsReadTicket | null> {
    const current = currentGeneration();
    if (!mounted.current || generation.current !== current) return null;
    const ticket = beginOptionsRead();
    if (!ticket || !mounted.current || generation.current !== current || !isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) return null;
    onTicket?.(ticket);
    try {
      const fresh = await api.get<StartOptions>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/start-options`);
      if (mounted.current && generation.current === current && isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) {
        if (expectedAttemptId && (fresh.activeAttempt?.id === expectedAttemptId
          || fresh.recentAttempts.some((item) => item.id === expectedAttemptId))) clearAttemptError(expectedAttemptId);
        onOptions(fresh, ticket);
        return ticket;
      }
    } catch (cause) {
      if (mounted.current && generation.current === current && isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) {
        const message = operationError(cause, 'Não foi possível conferir o estado atual.');
        if (expectedAttemptId) publishAttemptError(expectedAttemptId, message);
        else setError(message);
        return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  async function consultState() {
    if (!attempt || sendLatch.current) return;
    const attemptAtStart = attempt;
    const optionsAtStart = options;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    if (!mounted.current || generation.current !== current) return;
    const ticket = beginOptionsRead();
    if (!ticket || !mounted.current || generation.current !== current || !isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) return;
    try {
      const state = await api.get<InitialAttempt>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/initial-messages/${attempt.id}`);
      if (mounted.current && generation.current === current && isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) {
        if (state.id !== attemptAtStart.id) return;
        rememberAttempt(state, optionsAtStart);
        setError(null); clearAttemptError(state.id); publishNotice(null);
        if (sendSnapshotRef.current?.attemptId === state.id && (state.state === 'SUCCEEDED' || state.state === 'REJECTED' || state.slotReleasedAt)) {
          clearSnapshot();
        }
        if (isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) await reloadOptions();
      }
    } catch (cause) { if (mounted.current && generation.current === current && isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) publishAttemptError(attemptAtStart.id, operationError(cause, 'Não foi possível consultar o estado.')); }
  }

  async function reconcileLocal() {
    if (!attempt || attempt.recoveryAction !== 'RECONCILE' || sendLatch.current) return;
    const attemptAtStart = attempt;
    const optionsAtStart = options;
    const snapshotAtStart = sendSnapshotRef.current;
    const action = beginAction();
    if (!action) return;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    let ownedTicket = action.ticket;
    sendLatch.current = true;
    setSendingOwner({ id: action.owner, ticket: ownedTicket }); setError(null);
    try {
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, ownedTicket, current)) return;
      const response = await api.post<InitialAttempt>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/initial-messages/${attempt.id}/reconcile`, {});
      if (response.id !== attemptAtStart.id) return;
      if (snapshotAtStart?.attemptId === response.id && ownsSnapshot(snapshotAtStart, current)
        && (response.state === 'SUCCEEDED' || response.state === 'REJECTED' || response.slotReleasedAt)) {
        clearSnapshot();
      }
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, ownedTicket, current)) return;
      rememberAttempt(response, optionsAtStart);
      const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
      if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
      publishAttemptNotice(response.id, response.state === 'SUCCEEDED' ? 'Registro local concluído; nenhuma nova chamada ao canal foi feita.' : 'Estado consultado; nenhuma nova chamada ao canal foi feita.');
      if (response.state === 'SUCCEEDED') onComplete();
    } catch (cause) { if (actionIsCurrent(action.owner, ownedTicket, current)) publishAttemptError(attemptAtStart.id, operationError(cause, 'Não foi possível concluir o registro local.')); }
    finally {
      if (actionSequence.current === action.owner) {
        sendLatch.current = false;
        if (mounted.current) setSendingOwner((value) => value?.id === action.owner && value.ticket === ownedTicket ? null : value);
      }
    }
  }

  async function acknowledgeRisk() {
    if (!attempt || !(attempt.recoveryAction === 'ACK_AFTER_DEADLINE' || attempt.riskAcknowledgementAvailable)
      || confirmedRiskAttemptId !== attempt.id || sendLatch.current) return;
    const attemptAtStart = attempt;
    const optionsAtStart = options;
    const snapshotAtStart = sendSnapshotRef.current;
    const action = beginAction();
    if (!action) return;
    const current = currentGeneration();
    let ownedTicket = action.ticket;
    sendLatch.current = true;
    setSendingOwner({ id: action.owner, ticket: ownedTicket }); setError(null);
    try {
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, ownedTicket, current)) return;
      const response = await api.postWithHeaders<InitialAttempt>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/initial-messages/${attempt.id}/acknowledge`, {}, {
        'X-Confirm-Duplication-Risk': 'true',
      });
      if (response.id !== attemptAtStart.id) return;
      if (snapshotAtStart?.attemptId === response.id && ownsSnapshot(snapshotAtStart, current) && response.slotReleasedAt) {
        clearSnapshot();
      }
      if (!actionIsCurrent(action.owner, ownedTicket, current)) return;
      rememberAttempt(response, optionsAtStart);
      setConfirmedRiskAttemptId((currentAttemptId) => currentAttemptId === response.id ? null : currentAttemptId);
      const refreshTicket = await reloadOptions(undefined, (ticket) => { ownedTicket = ticket; setActionTicket(action.owner, ticket); });
      if (!refreshTicket || !actionIsCurrent(action.owner, refreshTicket, current)) return;
      publishAttemptNotice(response.id, 'Bloqueio de reenvio reconhecido. Nenhum envio foi repetido.');
    } catch (cause) { if (actionIsCurrent(action.owner, ownedTicket, current)) publishAttemptError(attemptAtStart.id, operationError(cause, 'Não foi possível registrar a confirmação de risco.')); }
    finally {
      if (actionSequence.current === action.owner) {
        sendLatch.current = false;
        if (mounted.current) setSendingOwner((value) => value?.id === action.owner && value.ticket === ownedTicket ? null : value);
      }
    }
  }

  if (locked && attempt) return <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Recuperação do primeiro envio">
    <strong>{attemptMessage(attempt)}</strong>
    {attempt.eligibleAt && <span>Confirmação de risco disponível após {new Date(attempt.eligibleAt).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}.</span>}
    {visibleNotice && <span role="status">{visibleNotice}</span>}
    <button type="button" onClick={() => void consultState()} disabled={sending}>Consultar estado</button>
    {attempt.recoveryAction === 'RECONCILE' && <button type="button" onClick={() => void reconcileLocal()} disabled={sending}>Concluir registro local</button>}
    {(attempt.recoveryAction === 'ACK_AFTER_DEADLINE' || attempt.riskAcknowledgementAvailable) && <>
      <label><input type="checkbox" checked={confirmedRiskAttemptId === attempt.id}
        onChange={(event) => setConfirmedRiskAttemptId(event.target.checked ? attempt.id : null)} />Confirmo o risco de mensagem duplicada.</label>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => void acknowledgeRisk()}
        disabled={sending || confirmedRiskAttemptId !== attempt.id}>Reconhecer risco sem reenviar</button>
    </>}
  </section>;

  return <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Primeira mensagem">
    <h2>Primeira mensagem</h2>
    <label>Canal selecionado
      <select value={channelId} onChange={(event) => { invalidateDraft(); setCatalogBusy(false); setChannelId(event.target.value); setSelectedTemplate(''); }} disabled={storageBlocked || retryOnly || sending}>
        <option value="">Selecione um canal</option>
        {options.channels.map((item) => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{channelLabel(item.type)}</option>)}
      </select>
    </label>
    {options.channels.length === 0 && <span role="status">Nenhum canal está disponível para iniciar o contato.</span>}
    {kindUnavailableMessage && <span role="status">{kindUnavailableMessage}</span>}
    <label>Tipo de primeira mensagem
      <select value={kind} onChange={(event) => { invalidateDraft(); setCatalogBusy(false); setKind(event.target.value as typeof kind); setSelectedTemplate(''); setParameterValues({}); }} disabled={storageBlocked || !channel || retryOnly || sending}>
        <option value="text">Texto</option>
        {channel?.supportedKinds.includes('approved-template') && <option value="approved-template">Modelo aprovado</option>}
      </select>
    </label>
    {kind === 'text' ? <label>Mensagem inicial
      <textarea value={text} onChange={(event) => { invalidateDraft(); setText([...event.target.value].slice(0, 4096).join('')); }} disabled={storageBlocked || retryOnly || sending} />
    </label> : <>
      <label>Modelo do catálogo
        <select value={selectedTemplate} onChange={(event) => { invalidateDraft(); setSelectedTemplate(event.target.value); setParameterValues({}); }} disabled={storageBlocked || retryOnly || sending}>
          <option value="">Selecione um modelo observado</option>
          {catalog.map((item) => <option key={templateKey(item)} value={templateKey(item)}>
            {item.name ?? item.remoteTemplateId} · {item.language ?? 'idioma desconhecido'} · {item.status}
          </option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      {selectedSummary && parameterNames.map((name) => <label key={name}>Parâmetro {name}
        <input value={parameterValues[name] ?? ''}
          onChange={(event) => { invalidateDraft(); setParameterValues((values) => ({ ...values, [name]: [...event.target.value].slice(0, 1024).join('') })); }} disabled={storageBlocked || retryOnly || sending} />
      </label>)}
      <div className="linha">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void refreshCatalog()} disabled={storageBlocked || catalogBusy || retryOnly || sending}>{catalogBusy ? 'Consultando…' : 'Atualizar catálogo'}</button>
        {catalogAfter && <button type="button" onClick={() => void loadMoreTemplates()} disabled={storageBlocked || catalogBusy || retryOnly || sending}>Mais modelos</button>}
      </div>
    </>}
    {storageReadUnavailable && <button type="button" onClick={restoreSnapshot} disabled={sending}>Recuperar tentativa salva</button>}
    {visibleNotice && <span role="status">{visibleNotice}</span>}
    <div className="linha">
      {onCancel && <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} disabled={sending || previewing}>Voltar ao histórico</button>}
      <button type="button" onClick={() => void previewMessage()} disabled={storageBlocked || retryOnly || previewing || sending || kindUnavailable || (kind === 'text' && !text.trim()) || (kind === 'approved-template' && (!selectedSummary || parameterNames.some((name) => !(parameterValues[name] ?? '').trim())))}>
        {previewing ? 'Pré-visualizando…' : 'Pré-visualizar'}
      </button>
      <button type="button" className="primario" onClick={() => void sendMessage()} disabled={storageBlocked || locked || sending || previewing || (!retryOnly && (kindUnavailable || !preview)) || (retryOnly && !sendSnapshot)}>
        {sending ? 'Enviando…' : retryOnly ? 'Retomar tentativa original' : 'Confirmar primeiro envio'}
      </button>
    </div>
    {preview && <div className="aviso" aria-label="Prévia da primeira mensagem">
      <strong>Prévia segura</strong><p>{preview.text}</p>
      {preview.channel.requiresWindow && <span>O canal exige janela atual para texto livre.</span>}
    </div>}
    {retryOnly && sendSnapshot && <span role="status">O resultado ainda não foi confirmado. Consulte a tentativa original antes de enviar outra mensagem.</span>}
    {locked && <span role="status">Há outra operação ativa; nenhuma nova mensagem será enviada.</span>}
  </section>;
}

export function OrdinaryRecovery({ conversationId, admission, onOptions, beginOptionsRead, isOptionsReadCurrent, invalidateOptionsReads, onAlert, onClearAlert }: {
  conversationId: string;
  admission: StartOptions['ordinaryAdmissions'][number];
  onOptions: (options: StartOptions, ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => void;
  beginOptionsRead: () => StartOptionsReadTicket | null;
  isOptionsReadCurrent: (ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => boolean;
  invalidateOptionsReads: () => void;
  onAlert: (source: string, message: string) => number;
  onClearAlert: (source: string, version: number) => void;
}) {
  const [confirmRisk, setConfirmRisk] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  const actionOwner = useRef(0);
  const actionLatch = useRef(false);
  const actionMounted = useRef(true);
  const alertVersion = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const alertSource = `ordinary-recovery:${conversationId}:${admission.id}`;
  useEffect(() => {
    actionMounted.current = true;
    actionOwner.current += 1;
    return () => { actionMounted.current = false; actionOwner.current += 1; };
  }, [conversationId, admission.id]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (error) alertVersion.current = onAlert(alertSource, error);
    else if (alertVersion.current !== null) { onClearAlert(alertSource, alertVersion.current); alertVersion.current = null; }
  }, [error, alertSource, onAlert, onClearAlert]);
  useEffect(() => () => { if (alertVersion.current !== null) onClearAlert(alertSource, alertVersion.current); }, [alertSource, onClearAlert]);
  const eligibleAt = admission.dispatchDeadlineAt ? Date.parse(admission.dispatchDeadlineAt) : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  const actionIsCurrent = (owner: number) => actionMounted.current && actionOwner.current === owner;
  const refresh = async (owner: number): Promise<{ ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket; kind: 'success' } | { ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket; kind: 'error'; error: unknown } | null> => {
    if (!actionIsCurrent(owner)) return null;
    const ticket = beginOptionsRead();
    if (!ticket || !actionIsCurrent(owner) || !isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) return null;
    let options: StartOptions;
    try { options = await api.get<StartOptions>(`/conversations/${conversationId}/start-options`); }
    catch (error) {
      return actionIsCurrent(owner) && isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket) ? { ticket, kind: 'error', error } : null;
    }
    if (!actionIsCurrent(owner) || !isOptionsReadCurrent(ticket)) return null;
    onOptions(options, ticket);
    return { ticket, kind: 'success' };
  };
  async function reconcile() {
    if (actionLatch.current) return;
    actionLatch.current = true;
    const owner = actionOwner.current;
    if (!actionIsCurrent(owner)) { actionLatch.current = false; return; }
    invalidateOptionsReads();
    setBusy(true); setNotice(null);
    try {
      try { await api.post(`/conversations/${conversationId}/dispatch-admissions/${admission.id}/reconcile`, {}); }
      catch (cause) { if (actionIsCurrent(owner)) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível consultar o estado.'); return; }
      const refreshed = await refresh(owner);
      if (!actionIsCurrent(owner) || !refreshed || !isOptionsReadCurrent(refreshed.ticket)) return;
      if (refreshed.kind === 'error') { setError(refreshed.error instanceof ErroDaApi ? refreshed.error.message : 'Não foi possível consultar o estado.'); return; }
      setError(null); setNotice('Estado atualizado sem repetir o envio.');
    }
    finally { actionLatch.current = false; if (actionMounted.current && actionOwner.current === owner) setBusy(false); }
  }
  async function acknowledge() {
    if (actionLatch.current || !confirmRisk || now < eligibleAt) return;
    actionLatch.current = true;
    const owner = actionOwner.current;
    if (!actionIsCurrent(owner)) { actionLatch.current = false; return; }
    invalidateOptionsReads();
    setBusy(true); setNotice(null);
    try {
      try {
        await api.postWithHeaders(`/conversations/${conversationId}/dispatch-admissions/${admission.id}/acknowledge`, {}, {
          'X-Confirm-Duplication-Risk': 'true',
        });
      } catch (cause) { if (actionIsCurrent(owner)) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível registrar a confirmação de risco.'); return; }
      const refreshed = await refresh(owner);
      if (!actionIsCurrent(owner) || !refreshed || !isOptionsReadCurrent(refreshed.ticket)) return;
      if (refreshed.kind === 'error') { setError(refreshed.error instanceof ErroDaApi ? refreshed.error.message : 'Não foi possível registrar a confirmação de risco.'); return; }
      setError(null);
      setConfirmRisk(false);
      setNotice('O bloqueio foi liberado após a confirmação de risco. Nenhum envio foi repetido.');
    }
    finally { actionLatch.current = false; if (actionMounted.current && actionOwner.current === owner) setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Recuperação do envio comum">
    <strong>O resultado do envio comum ainda não está confirmado. Consulte o estado antes de responder.</strong>
    {admission.dispatchDeadlineAt && <span>Confirmação de risco disponível após {new Date(admission.dispatchDeadlineAt).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}.</span>}
    {notice && <span role="status">{notice}</span>}
    <button type="button" onClick={() => void reconcile()} disabled={busy}>Consultar estado</button>
    <label><input type="checkbox" checked={confirmRisk} onChange={(event) => setConfirmRisk(event.target.checked)} />Confirmo o risco de mensagem duplicada.</label>
    <button type="button" onClick={() => void acknowledge()} disabled={busy || !confirmRisk || now < eligibleAt}>Reconhecer risco sem reenviar</button>
  </section>;
}


## FULL SOURCE BODY web-app/src/telas/Inbox.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { BusinessUnit, Conversa, ErroDaApi, Eu, Mensagem, Thread, api, ignorarSessaoMorta } from '../api';
import { ouvirTempoReal } from '../tempoReal';
import { cruzouPiso, mesclarMensagens } from '../timeline';
import { NovaConversa, OrdinaryRecovery, PrimeiraMensagem, type StartOptions, type StartOptionsReadTicket } from '../componentes/NovaConversa';
import InstagramReplyComposer, { InstagramDraftReview } from '../componentes/InstagramReplyComposer';
import { clearVersionedAlert, createVersionedAlert } from '../instagramState';

const ABAS = [
  { id: 'QUEUED', rotulo: 'Na fila' },
  { id: 'IN_SERVICE', rotulo: 'Em atendimento' },
  { id: 'CLOSED', rotulo: 'Finalizadas' },
  { id: 'GRUPO', rotulo: 'Grupos' },
];

function explicarMotivoEnvio(motivo: string | null): string {
  switch (motivo) {
    case 'CANAL_AUSENTE': return 'Não há um canal conectado para responder.';
    case 'CANAL_AMBIGUO': return 'Esta conversa ainda não tem um canal definido. Aguarde uma mensagem recebida para responder.';
    case 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL': return 'O canal desta conversa não está disponível.';
    case 'CHANNEL_IDENTITY_NOT_VERIFIED': return 'A identidade do canal ainda precisa ser verificada.';
    case 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY': return 'O canal ainda não está pronto para enviar.';
    case 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL': return 'A credencial do canal precisa ser atualizada.';
    case 'JANELA_FECHADA': return 'A janela de 24 horas foi encerrada.';
    default: return 'O envio está temporariamente indisponível.';
  }
}

function rotuloDaConta(display: Conversa['display']): string {
  const label = display?.accountLabel?.trim();
  if (label) return label;
  if (display?.platform === 'INSTAGRAM') return 'Instagram';
  if (display?.platform === 'WHATSAPP') return 'WhatsApp';
  return 'Canal não identificado';
}

interface Recuperacao {
  id: string;
  actorId: string;
  geracao: number;
  piso: string;
  cursor: string | null;
  dirty: boolean;
  eventos: number;
  emVoo: boolean;
  paginasRestantes: number;
  vazio?: boolean;
}

interface InvalidacaoDeAbertura {
  id: string;
  geracao: number;
  eventos: number;
}

interface ErroDeRecuperacao {
  geracao: number;
  versao: number;
}

type AlertaAtual = ReturnType<typeof createVersionedAlert>;

export default function Inbox({ eu }: { eu: Eu }) {
  const [bus, setBus] = useState<BusinessUnit[]>([]);
  const [buId, setBuId] = useState<string>('');
  const [aba, setAba] = useState('QUEUED');
  const [conversas, setConversas] = useState<Conversa[]>([]);
  const [paginaLista, setPaginaLista] = useState(1);
  const [totalLista, setTotalLista] = useState(0);
  const [temMaisLista, setTemMaisLista] = useState(false);
  const [carregandoLista, setCarregandoLista] = useState(false);
  const [erroLista, setErroLista] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [aberta, setAberta] = useState<Thread | null>(null);
  const [abrindo, setAbrindo] = useState(false);
  const [alertaAtual, setAlertaAtual] = useState<AlertaAtual | null>(null);
  const versaoAlerta = useRef(0);
  const [carregandoAnteriores, setCarregandoAnteriores] = useState(false);
  const [recuperacaoPendente, setRecuperacaoPendente] = useState(false);
  const [novaConversa, setNovaConversa] = useState(false);
  const [startOptions, setStartOptions] = useState<StartOptions | null>(null);
  const [startOptionsFor, setStartOptionsFor] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [startOptionsError, setStartOptionsError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [manualInitialConversationId, setManualInitialConversationId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [metadataReconnectEpoch, setMetadataReconnectEpoch] = useState(0);
  const metadataReconnectEpochRef = useRef(0);
  metadataReconnectEpochRef.current = metadataReconnectEpoch;
  const startOptionsIntent = useRef(0);
  const startOptionsOwner = useRef<{ ticket: number; actorId: string; buId: string; conversationId: string; generation: number; navigation: number } | null>(null);
  const startOptionsErrorReconnectEpoch = useRef(0);
  const versaoErro = useRef(0);
  const erroRecuperacao = useRef<ErroDeRecuperacao | null>(null);
  const geracao = useRef(0);
  const navigationIntent = useRef(0);
  const listaIntent = useRef(0);
  const bootstrapIntent = useRef(0);
  const mounted = useRef(true);
  const actorAtual = useRef(eu.id);
  const actorForState = useRef(eu.id);
  actorAtual.current = eu.id;
  const buAtual = useRef('');
  const conversaAtual = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const abaAtual = useRef('QUEUED');
  const threadAtual = useRef<Thread | null>(null);
  const recuperacao = useRef<Recuperacao | null>(null);
  const invalidacaoAbertura = useRef<InvalidacaoDeAbertura | null>(null);
  const executarRecuperacaoRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(async () => {});
  const versaoMetadados = useRef(0);
  const bolhas = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const carregarListaRef = useRef<(id?: string, filtro?: string) => Promise<void>>(async () => {});
  const reconciliarRef = useRef<() => void>(() => {});

  const publicarAlerta = useCallback((source: string, message: string): number => {
    const version = ++versaoAlerta.current;
    setAlertaAtual(createVersionedAlert(source, version, message));
    return version;
  }, []);
  const limparAlerta = useCallback((source: string, version: number) => {
    setAlertaAtual((current) => clearVersionedAlert(current, source, version));
  }, []);
  const erroOpcoesAlerta = useRef<{ source: string; version: number } | null>(null);
  const publicarErroOpcoes = useCallback((actor: string, bu: string, conversation: string, generation: number, navigation: number, message: string) => {
    const source = `start-options:${actor}:${bu}:${conversation}:${generation}:${navigation}`;
    erroOpcoesAlerta.current = { source, version: publicarAlerta(source, message) };
  }, [publicarAlerta]);
  const limparErroOpcoes = useCallback((actor: string, bu: string, conversation: string, generation: number, navigation: number, expected?: { source: string; version: number } | null) => {
    const source = `start-options:${actor}:${bu}:${conversation}:${generation}:${navigation}`;
    const owned = expected === undefined ? erroOpcoesAlerta.current : expected;
    if (owned?.source === source && erroOpcoesAlerta.current?.source === source && erroOpcoesAlerta.current.version === owned.version) {
      limparAlerta(source, owned.version);
      erroOpcoesAlerta.current = null;
    }
  }, [limparAlerta]);
  const invalidateStartOptionsReads = useCallback(() => { ++startOptionsIntent.current; startOptionsOwner.current = null; }, []);
  const beginStartOptionsRead = useCallback((actorId: string, businessUnitId: string, conversationId: string, generation: number, navigation: number): StartOptionsReadTicket | null => {
    if (actorId !== actorAtual.current || businessUnitId !== buAtual.current || conversationId !== conversaAtual.current
      || generation !== geracao.current || navigation !== navigationIntent.current) return null;
    const owner = { ticket: ++startOptionsIntent.current, actorId, buId: businessUnitId, conversationId, generation, navigation };
    startOptionsOwner.current = owner;
    return owner;
  }, []);
  const startOptionsTicketIsCurrent = useCallback((ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket) => {
    const owner = startOptionsOwner.current;
    return owner?.ticket === ticket.ticket && owner.actorId === ticket.actorId && owner.buId === ticket.buId
      && owner.conversationId === ticket.conversationId && owner.generation === ticket.generation && owner.navigation === ticket.navigation
      && ticket.actorId === actorAtual.current && ticket.actorId === actorForState.current && ticket.buId === buAtual.current
      && ticket.conversationId === conversaAtual.current && ticket.generation === geracao.current && ticket.navigation === navigationIntent.current;
  }, []);
  const publishStartOptions = useCallback((ticket: StartOptionsReadTicket, result: StartOptions) => {
    if (!startOptionsTicketIsCurrent(ticket)) return;
    const ownOptionsError = erroOpcoesAlerta.current;
    // Preserve the accepted response object identity: child recovery facts captured
    // against an older snapshot become ineligible as soon as this owner advances.
    setStartOptions(result); setStartOptionsFor(ticket.conversationId); setStartOptionsError(null);
    limparErroOpcoes(ticket.actorId, ticket.buId, ticket.conversationId, ticket.generation, ticket.navigation, ownOptionsError);
  }, [limparErroOpcoes, startOptionsTicketIsCurrent]);

  const guardarThread = (thread: Thread | null) => {
    threadAtual.current = thread;
    setAberta(thread);
  };

  const definirErro = useCallback((mensagem: string | null, geracaoDaRecuperacao?: number) => {
    versaoErro.current += 1;
    erroRecuperacao.current = mensagem !== null && geracaoDaRecuperacao !== undefined
      ? { geracao: geracaoDaRecuperacao, versao: versaoErro.current }
      : null;
    if (mensagem) publicarAlerta('history', mensagem);
    else setAlertaAtual((current) => current?.source === 'history' ? null : current);
  }, [publicarAlerta]);

  const limparErroRecuperacao = useCallback((geracaoDaRecuperacao: number) => {
    const erroAtual = erroRecuperacao.current;
    if (erroAtual?.geracao !== geracaoDaRecuperacao || erroAtual.versao !== versaoErro.current) return;
    erroRecuperacao.current = null;
    versaoErro.current += 1;
    setAlertaAtual((current) => current?.source === 'history' ? null : current);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    let active = true;
    const actor = eu.id;
    const previousActor = actorForState.current;
    if (previousActor !== actor) {
      invalidateStartOptionsReads();
      geracao.current += 1;
      navigationIntent.current += 1;
      ++listaIntent.current;
      buAtual.current = '';
      conversaAtual.current = null;
      recuperacao.current = null;
      invalidacaoAbertura.current = null;
      guardarThread(null);
      setAbrindo(false);
      setConversas([]);
      setPaginaLista(1);
      setTotalLista(0);
      setTemMaisLista(false);
      setErroLista(null);
      setBus([]);
      setBuId('');
      abaAtual.current = 'QUEUED';
      setAba('QUEUED');
      setNovaConversa(false);
      setStartOptions(null);
      setStartOptionsFor(null);
      setStartOptionsError(null);
      setAlertaAtual(null);
    }
    actorForState.current = actor;
    actorAtual.current = actor;
    const ticket = ++bootstrapIntent.current;
    api.get<{ businessUnits: BusinessUnit[] }>('/business-units').then((r) => {
      if (!active || !mounted.current || actor !== actorAtual.current || ticket !== bootstrapIntent.current) return;
      const lista = r.businessUnits ?? [];
      setBus(lista);
      const inicial = lista[0]?.id ?? '';
      buAtual.current = inicial;
      setBuId(inicial);
    }).catch((error) => { if (active && mounted.current && actor === actorAtual.current && ticket === bootstrapIntent.current) ignorarSessaoMorta(error); });
    return () => { active = false; mounted.current = false; ++bootstrapIntent.current; ++listaIntent.current; };
  }, [eu.id]);

  const carregarLista = useCallback(async (id = buAtual.current, filtro = aba, pagina = paginaLista) => {
    if (!id || actorForState.current !== eu.id || buAtual.current !== id) return;
    const ticket = ++listaIntent.current;
    const navigation = geracao.current;
    const navigationTicket = navigationIntent.current;
    const actor = eu.id;
    setCarregandoLista(true);
    setErroLista(null);
    const stillCurrent = () => mounted.current && actorForState.current === actor && actor === actorAtual.current && ticket === listaIntent.current
      && navigation === geracao.current && navigationTicket === navigationIntent.current && buAtual.current === id && abaAtual.current === filtro && pagina === paginaLista;
    try {
      const r = await api.get<{ conversations: Conversa[]; total: number; pagina: number; temMais: boolean }>(`/business-units/${id}/conversations?aba=${encodeURIComponent(filtro)}&pagina=${pagina}&limite=50`);
      if (stillCurrent()) {
        setConversas(r.conversations);
        setTotalLista(r.total);
        setPaginaLista(r.pagina);
        setTemMaisLista(r.temMais);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (stillCurrent()) setErroLista('Não foi possível atualizar a lista de conversas.');
    } finally {
      if (stillCurrent()) setCarregandoLista(false);
    }
  }, [aba, eu.id, paginaLista]);

  const aplicarPaginaRecente = useCallback((id: string, actorDaChamada: string, geracaoDaChamada: number, versao: number, response: Thread) => {
    if (actorAtual.current !== actorDaChamada || conversaAtual.current !== id || geracao.current !== geracaoDaChamada) return;
    const anterior = threadAtual.current;
    const novo: Thread = {
      ...response,
      messages: mesclarMensagens(anterior?.messages ?? [], response.messages),
      // O cursor de reconciliação recente não substitui o cursor do histórico.
      paginacao: anterior && anterior.messages.length > 0 ? anterior.paginacao : response.paginacao,
      conversa: versaoMetadados.current === versao ? response.conversa : anterior?.conversa ?? response.conversa,
      janela: versaoMetadados.current === versao ? response.janela : anterior?.janela ?? response.janela,
    };
    guardarThread(novo);
  }, []);

  const executarRecuperacao = useCallback(async () => {
    const r = recuperacao.current;
    if (!r || r.emVoo || r.paginasRestantes <= 0 || r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.geracao !== geracao.current || r.id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
    r.emVoo = true;
    const eventosNoInicio = r.eventos;
    r.dirty = false;
    let cruzou = false;
    try {
      if (r.vazio) {
        if (r.cursor === null && r.paginasRestantes > 0) {
          const versao = versaoMetadados.current;
          r.paginasRestantes -= 1;
          const inicial = await api.get<Thread>(`/conversations/${r.id}/messages?limite=50&marcarLida=false`);
          if (r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.geracao !== geracao.current || r.id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
          aplicarPaginaRecente(r.id, r.actorId, r.geracao, versao, inicial);
          r.cursor = inicial.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
          if (!inicial.paginacao.temAnteriores) r.cursor = null;
        }
        while (r.cursor && r.paginasRestantes > 0 && !r.dirty) {
          const cursor = r.cursor;
          r.paginasRestantes -= 1;
          const pagina = await api.get<Thread>(`/conversations/${r.id}/messages?limite=50&marcarLida=false&antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}`);
          if (r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.geracao !== geracao.current || r.id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
          const atual = threadAtual.current;
          if (atual) guardarThread({
            ...atual,
            messages: mesclarMensagens(atual.messages, pagina.messages),
            paginacao: pagina.paginacao,
          });
          r.cursor = pagina.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
        }
        if (r.eventos !== eventosNoInicio || r.dirty) {
          r.cursor = null;
          setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
        } else if (!r.cursor) {
          recuperacao.current = null;
          setRecuperacaoPendente(false);
          limparErroRecuperacao(r.geracao);
        } else {
          setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (r.cursor === null && r.paginasRestantes > 0) {
        const versao = versaoMetadados.current;
        r.paginasRestantes -= 1;
        const recente = await api.get<Thread>(`/conversations/${r.id}/messages?limite=50&marcarLida=false`);
        if (r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.geracao !== geracao.current || r.id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
        aplicarPaginaRecente(r.id, r.actorId, r.geracao, versao, recente);
        r.cursor = recente.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
        cruzou = cruzouPiso(recente.messages, r.piso) || !recente.paginacao.temAnteriores;
      }
      while (!cruzou && r.cursor && r.paginasRestantes > 0 && !r.dirty) {
        const cursor = r.cursor;
        r.paginasRestantes -= 1;
        const pagina = await api.get<Thread>(`/conversations/${r.id}/messages?limite=50&marcarLida=false&antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}`);
        if (r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.geracao !== geracao.current || r.id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
        const atual = threadAtual.current;
        if (atual) guardarThread({
          ...atual,
          messages: mesclarMensagens(atual.messages, pagina.messages),
        });
        r.cursor = pagina.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
        cruzou = cruzouPiso(pagina.messages, r.piso) || !pagina.paginacao.temAnteriores;
      }

      if (r.eventos !== eventosNoInicio || r.dirty) {
        // Um evento concorrente exige nova passagem recente e preserva o piso.
        r.cursor = null;
        setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
      } else if (cruzou) {
