        recuperacao.current = null;
        setRecuperacaoPendente(false);
        limparErroRecuperacao(r.geracao);
      } else {
        setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (r.actorId === actorAtual.current && r.geracao === geracao.current && r.id === conversaAtual.current) {
        if (r.dirty || r.eventos !== eventosNoInicio) r.cursor = null;
        definirErro(e instanceof ErroDaApi && e.status === 404 ? 'Acesso à conversa perdido.' : 'Não foi possível atualizar o histórico. Tente novamente.', r.geracao);
        setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
      }
    } finally {
      r.emVoo = false;
      if (recuperacao.current === r && r.actorId === actorAtual.current && (r.dirty || r.eventos !== eventosNoInicio) && r.paginasRestantes > 0 && r.geracao === geracao.current) {
        queueMicrotask(() => void executarRecuperacaoRef.current());
      }
    }
  }, [aplicarPaginaRecente, definirErro, limparErroRecuperacao]);

  useEffect(() => { executarRecuperacaoRef.current = executarRecuperacao; }, [executarRecuperacao]);

  const reconciliar = useCallback(() => {
    const thread = threadAtual.current;
    const id = conversaAtual.current;
    if (!id) return;
    if (!thread) {
      const abertura = invalidacaoAbertura.current;
      if (abertura?.id === id && abertura.geracao === geracao.current) abertura.eventos += 1;
      return;
    }
    let r = recuperacao.current;
    if (!r || r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.id !== id || r.geracao !== geracao.current) {
      const mensagens = thread.messages;
      if (mensagens.length === 0) {
        r = { id, actorId: eu.id, geracao: geracao.current, piso: '', cursor: null, dirty: true, eventos: 0, emVoo: false, paginasRestantes: 3, vazio: true };
        recuperacao.current = r;
        void executarRecuperacao();
        return;
      }
      const piso = mensagens.reduce((max, m) => Date.parse(m.createdAt) > Date.parse(max) ? m.createdAt : max, mensagens[0].createdAt);
      r = { id, actorId: eu.id, geracao: geracao.current, piso, cursor: null, dirty: true, eventos: 0, emVoo: false, paginasRestantes: 3 };
      recuperacao.current = r;
      setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
    } else {
      r.dirty = true;
      r.eventos += 1;
      if (!r.emVoo) r.cursor = null;
      setRecuperacaoPendente(true);
    }
    if (r.paginasRestantes > 0) void executarRecuperacao();
  }, [aplicarPaginaRecente, executarRecuperacao, eu.id]);

  const iniciarRodadaAutorizada = useCallback(() => {
    const r = recuperacao.current;
    if (!r || r.emVoo || r.actorId !== actorAtual.current || r.geracao !== geracao.current || r.id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
    r.paginasRestantes = 3;
    void executarRecuperacao();
  }, [executarRecuperacao]);

  useEffect(() => {
    carregarListaRef.current = carregarLista;
    reconciliarRef.current = reconciliar;
  }, [carregarLista, reconciliar]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = aberta?.conversa.id;
    if (!id) {
      setStartOptions(null);
      setStartOptionsFor(null);
      setStartOptionsError(null);
      return;
    }
    let current = true;
    const actorAtStart = eu.id;
    const generationAtStart = geracao.current;
    const navigationAtStart = navigationIntent.current;
    const reconnectEpochAtStart = metadataReconnectEpochRef.current;
    const ticket = beginStartOptionsRead(actorAtStart, buId, id, generationAtStart, navigationAtStart);
    if (!ticket) return;
    setStartOptions(null);
    setStartOptionsFor(null);
    setStartOptionsError(null);
    api.get<StartOptions>(`/conversations/${id}/start-options`).then((result) => {
      if (!current || !startOptionsTicketIsCurrent(ticket)) return;
      publishStartOptions(ticket, result);
    }).catch((cause) => {
      if (!current || !startOptionsTicketIsCurrent(ticket)) return;
      const message = cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível conferir as opções de envio.';
      startOptionsErrorReconnectEpoch.current = reconnectEpochAtStart;
      setStartOptionsError(message);
      publicarErroOpcoes(actorAtStart, buId, id, generationAtStart, navigationAtStart, message);
      setStartOptionsFor(id);
    });
    return () => { current = false; if (startOptionsOwner.current?.ticket === ticket.ticket) invalidateStartOptionsReads(); };
  }, [aberta?.conversa.id, buId, aba, eu.id, beginStartOptionsRead, invalidateStartOptionsReads, publicarErroOpcoes, publishStartOptions, startOptionsTicketIsCurrent]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = aberta?.conversa.id;
    if (!id || !startOptionsError || metadataReconnectEpoch <= startOptionsErrorReconnectEpoch.current) return;
    const actor = eu.id;
    const bu = buId;
    const generation = geracao.current;
    const navigation = navigationIntent.current;
    const ticket = beginStartOptionsRead(actor, bu, id, generation, navigation);
    if (!ticket) return;
    const stillCurrent = () => mounted.current && startOptionsTicketIsCurrent(ticket)
      && actor === actorAtual.current && generation === geracao.current && navigation === navigationIntent.current
      && id === conversaAtual.current && bu === buAtual.current;
    void api.get<StartOptions>(`/conversations/${id}/start-options`).then((result) => {
      if (!stillCurrent()) return;
      publishStartOptions(ticket, result);
    }).catch(() => {
      // Keep the current error and all composer state until another real reconnect.
      if (!stillCurrent()) return;
      startOptionsErrorReconnectEpoch.current = metadataReconnectEpoch;
    });
  }, [metadataReconnectEpoch, startOptionsError, aberta?.conversa.id, buId, eu.id, beginStartOptionsRead, publishStartOptions, startOptionsTicketIsCurrent]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregarLista().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
    const t = setInterval(() => {
      void carregarLista().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
      if (recuperacao.current) iniciarRodadaAutorizada();
      else reconciliar();
    }, 60_000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [buId, aba, carregarLista, reconciliar, iniciarRodadaAutorizada]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Espera a lista inicial de BUs e mantém o socket apesar de filtros, BUs ou
    // callbacks locais mudarem. Isso evita reconectar durante o mesmo login.
    if (bus.length === 0) return;
    const actor = eu.id;
    const parar = ouvirTempoReal((evento) => {
      if (actor !== actorAtual.current) return;
      if (evento.buId !== buAtual.current) return;
      void carregarListaRef.current().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
      if (evento.conversaId === conversaAtual.current) reconciliarRef.current();
    }, () => { setMetadataReconnectEpoch((value) => value + 1); reconciliarRef.current(); });
    return parar;
  }, [eu.id, bus.length]);

  function trocarBu(novoBu: string) {
    invalidateStartOptionsReads();
    geracao.current += 1;
    navigationIntent.current += 1;
    ++listaIntent.current;
    buAtual.current = novoBu;
    conversaAtual.current = null;
    recuperacao.current = null;
    invalidacaoAbertura.current = null;
    guardarThread(null);
    setAbrindo(false);
    setCarregandoAnteriores(false);
    definirErro(null);
    setAlertaAtual(null);
    setRecuperacaoPendente(false);
    setConversas([]);
    setPaginaLista(1);
    setTotalLista(0);
    setTemMaisLista(false);
    setErroLista(null);
    setStartOptions(null);
    setStartOptionsFor(null);
    setStartOptionsError(null);
    setManualInitialConversationId(null);
    setBuId(novoBu);
  }

  async function abrir(id: string, marcarLida = true) {
    invalidateStartOptionsReads();
    const gen = ++geracao.current;
    const nav = ++navigationIntent.current;
    const actor = eu.id;
    conversaAtual.current = id;
    recuperacao.current = null;
    invalidacaoAbertura.current = { id, geracao: gen, eventos: 0 };
    versaoMetadados.current += 1;
    guardarThread(null);
    setAbrindo(true);
    setCarregandoAnteriores(false);
    definirErro(null);
    setAlertaAtual(null);
    setRecuperacaoPendente(false);
    try {
      const response = await api.get<Thread>(`/conversations/${id}/messages?marcarLida=${marcarLida}`);
      if (actor !== actorAtual.current || gen !== geracao.current || nav !== navigationIntent.current || conversaAtual.current !== id) return;
      response.messages = mesclarMensagens(response.messages);
      guardarThread(response);
      setAbrindo(false);
      const invalidacao = invalidacaoAbertura.current;
      invalidacaoAbertura.current = null;
      void carregarLista().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
      if (invalidacao?.id === id && invalidacao.geracao === gen && invalidacao.eventos > 0) reconciliar();
    } catch (e) {
      if (actor === actorAtual.current && gen === geracao.current && nav === navigationIntent.current) {
        invalidacaoAbertura.current = null;
        guardarThread(null);
        setAbrindo(false);
        definirErro(e instanceof ErroDaApi && e.status === 404 ? 'Acesso à conversa perdido.' : 'Não foi possível carregar a conversa.');
      }
    }
  }

  async function carregarAnteriores() {
    const atual = threadAtual.current;
    const id = conversaAtual.current;
    const cursor = atual?.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
    if (!atual || !id || !cursor || carregandoAnteriores) return;
    const gen = geracao.current;
    const actor = eu.id;
    setCarregandoAnteriores(true);
    definirErro(null);
    try {
      const pagina = await api.get<Thread>(`/conversations/${id}/messages?antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}`);
      if (actor !== actorAtual.current || gen !== geracao.current || id !== conversaAtual.current || !threadAtual.current) return;
      // Inclua alterações legítimas que chegaram enquanto a página estava no ar.
      const alturaAntes = bolhas.current?.scrollHeight ?? 0;
      const topoAntes = bolhas.current?.scrollTop ?? 0;
      const corrente = threadAtual.current;
      guardarThread({ ...corrente, messages: mesclarMensagens(corrente.messages, pagina.messages), paginacao: pagina.paginacao });
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (actor === actorAtual.current && gen === geracao.current && bolhas.current) bolhas.current.scrollTop = topoAntes + (bolhas.current.scrollHeight - alturaAntes);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (actor === actorAtual.current && gen === geracao.current) definirErro(e instanceof ErroDaApi && e.status === 404 ? 'Acesso à conversa perdido.' : 'Falha ao carregar mensagens anteriores.');
    } finally {
      if (actor === actorAtual.current && gen === geracao.current) setCarregandoAnteriores(false);
    }
  }

  async function agir(acao: () => Promise<unknown>) {
    invalidateStartOptionsReads();
    const id = conversaAtual.current;
    const gen = geracao.current;
    const actor = eu.id;
    if (!id) return;
    versaoMetadados.current += 1;
    definirErro(null);
    try {
      await acao();
      if (actor !== actorAtual.current || gen !== geracao.current || id !== conversaAtual.current) return;
      void carregarLista().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
      reconciliar();
    } catch (e) {
      if (actor === actorAtual.current && gen === geracao.current) definirErro(e instanceof ErroDaApi ? e.message : 'Falhou.');
    }
  }

  const envioIndisponivel = aberta ? !aberta.janela.disponivel : false;
  const janelaFechada = aberta?.janela.exigida && !aberta.janela.aberta;
  const currentStartOptions = aberta && startOptionsFor === aberta.conversa.id && !startOptionsError ? startOptions : null;
  const ordinaryUncertainAdmission = currentStartOptions?.ordinaryAdmissions.find((item) => ['SENDING', 'UNCERTAIN'].includes(item.state)) ?? null;
  const activeOrdinaryAdmission = Boolean(ordinaryUncertainAdmission);
  const requiresInitialFlow = Boolean(aberta && (currentStartOptions?.activeAttempt
    || (aberta.messages.length === 0 && currentStartOptions?.initialRecipientPlatform === 'WHATSAPP')
    || manualInitialConversationId === aberta.conversa.id));
  const startOptionsLoading = Boolean(aberta && startOptionsFor !== aberta.conversa.id);
  const startOptionsUnresolved = Boolean(aberta && (startOptionsLoading || startOptionsError));
  const isInstagramConversation = aberta?.conversa.display?.platform === 'INSTAGRAM';
  const activeConversationId = aberta?.conversa.id;
  const activeGeneration = geracao.current;
  const activeNavigation = navigationIntent.current;
  const actorAtRender = eu.id;
  const refreshOrdinaryOptions = async (id: string, generationAtRender: number, buAtRender: string, navigationAtRender: number) => {
    const actor = eu.id;
    const reconnectEpochAtStart = metadataReconnectEpochRef.current;
    const ticket = beginStartOptionsRead(actor, buAtRender, id, generationAtRender, navigationAtRender);
    if (!ticket) return;
    try {
      publishStartOptions(ticket, await api.get<StartOptions>(`/conversations/${id}/start-options`));
    } catch (cause) {
      if (!startOptionsTicketIsCurrent(ticket)) return;
      const message = cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível conferir as opções de envio.';
      startOptionsErrorReconnectEpoch.current = reconnectEpochAtStart;
      setStartOptionsFor(id);
      setStartOptionsError(message);
      publicarErroOpcoes(actor, buAtRender, id, generationAtRender, navigationAtRender, message);
    }
  };
  const retryStartOptions = async (id: string, generation: number, navigation: number) => {
    const actor = eu.id;
    const reconnectEpochAtStart = metadataReconnectEpochRef.current;
    const ticket = beginStartOptionsRead(actor, buId, id, generation, navigation);
    if (!ticket) return;
    try {
      publishStartOptions(ticket, await api.get<StartOptions>(`/conversations/${id}/start-options`));
    } catch (cause) {
      if (!startOptionsTicketIsCurrent(ticket)) return;
      const message = cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível conferir as opções de envio.';
      startOptionsErrorReconnectEpoch.current = reconnectEpochAtStart;
      setStartOptionsError(message);
      publicarErroOpcoes(actor, buId, id, generation, navigation, message);
    }
  };
  const completeInitial = (id: string, generationAtRender: number, navigationAtRender: number) => {
    if (actorAtRender !== actorAtual.current || id !== conversaAtual.current || generationAtRender !== geracao.current || navigationAtRender !== navigationIntent.current) return;
    setManualInitialConversationId(null);
    void abrir(id, false);
  };
  const novaConversationActor = eu.id;
  const novaConversationBu = buId;
  const novaConversationGeneration = geracao.current;
  const novaConversationNavigation = navigationIntent.current;

  if (actorForState.current !== eu.id) return <div className="grade"><p className="suave" role="status">Carregando conversas do usuário atual…</p></div>;

  return (
    <div className="grade">
      <div className="linha">
        <select aria-label="Business Unit" value={buId} onChange={(e) => trocarBu(e.target.value)} style={{ maxWidth: 280 }}>
          {bus.map((b) => <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>{b.name}</option>)}
        </select>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setNovaConversa(true)} disabled={!buId}>Nova conversa</button>
        {alertaAtual && <span className="erro" role="alert" data-alert-version={alertaAtual.version}>{alertaAtual.message}</span>}
      </div>

      {novaConversa && <NovaConversa key={`${novaConversationActor}:${novaConversationBu}:${novaConversationGeneration}:${novaConversationNavigation}`} buId={buId} onAlert={publicarAlerta} onClearAlert={limparAlerta} onClose={() => {
        if (novaConversationActor === actorAtual.current && novaConversationBu === buAtual.current && novaConversationGeneration === geracao.current && novaConversationNavigation === navigationIntent.current) setNovaConversa(false);
      }} onOpened={(result) => {
        if (novaConversationActor !== actorAtual.current || novaConversationBu !== buAtual.current || novaConversationGeneration !== geracao.current || novaConversationNavigation !== navigationIntent.current) return;
        setNovaConversa(false);
        setManualInitialConversationId(result.conversaId);
        void carregarLista().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
        void abrir(result.conversaId, false);
      }} />}

      <div className="abas">
        {ABAS.map((a) => <button key={a.id} className={aba === a.id ? 'ativa' : ''} aria-pressed={aba === a.id} onClick={() => { invalidateStartOptionsReads(); navigationIntent.current += 1; ++listaIntent.current; abaAtual.current = a.id; setPaginaLista(1); setAba(a.id); }}>{a.rotulo}</button>)}
      </div>

      <div className="inbox">
        <div className="cartao lista" style={{ padding: 0 }}>
          <div className="linha" aria-label="Paginação de conversas">
            <button type="button" aria-label="Página anterior de conversas" disabled={paginaLista <= 1 || carregandoLista} onClick={() => { ++listaIntent.current; setPaginaLista((page) => Math.max(1, page - 1)); }}>Anterior</button>
            <span role="status">Página {paginaLista} · {totalLista} conversas{carregandoLista ? ' · Carregando…' : ''}</span>
            <button type="button" aria-label="Próxima página de conversas" disabled={!temMaisLista || carregandoLista} onClick={() => { ++listaIntent.current; setPaginaLista((page) => page + 1); }}>Próxima</button>
            <button type="button" aria-label="Atualizar lista de conversas" disabled={carregandoLista} onClick={() => void carregarLista(buAtual.current, abaAtual.current, paginaLista)}>Atualizar</button>
          </div>
          {erroLista && <p className="erro" role="alert">{erroLista}</p>}
          {conversas.map((c) => <button key={c.id} className={`item ${aberta?.conversa.id === c.id ? 'ativo' : ''}`} aria-label={`${c.display?.recipientLabel || 'Contato'} ${c.attendantName ?? 'sem atendente'} · ${rotuloDaConta(c.display)}`} onClick={() => { setManualInitialConversationId(null); void abrir(c.id); }}>
            <div className="linha"><strong>{c.display?.recipientLabel || 'Contato'}</strong>{c.unreadCount > 0 && <span className="suave">({c.unreadCount})</span>}</div>
            <div className="suave">{rotuloDaConta(c.display)}</div>
            <div className="suave">{c.attendantName ?? 'sem atendente'}</div>
          </button>)}
          {conversas.length === 0 && <p className="suave" style={{ padding: 12 }}>Nada aqui.</p>}
        </div>

        <div className="cartao grade">
          {!aberta && (abrindo
            ? <p className="suave" role="status" aria-live="polite">Abrindo conversa…</p>
            : <p className="suave">Escolha uma conversa.</p>)}
          {aberta && <>
            <div className="linha">
              <strong>{aberta.conversa.display?.recipientLabel || 'Contato'}</strong>
              <span className="suave">{aberta.conversa.status}</span>
              <span className="suave" style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }}>{rotuloDaConta(aberta.conversa.display)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="linha">
              {aberta.paginacao.temAnteriores
                ? <button onClick={() => void carregarAnteriores()} disabled={carregandoAnteriores} aria-label="Carregar mensagens anteriores">
                    {carregandoAnteriores ? 'Carregando…' : 'Carregar anteriores'}
                  </button>
                : <span className="suave">Início do histórico</span>}
              {recuperacaoPendente && <span className="suave" role="status">Atualização pendente. O histórico pode ter uma lacuna.</span>}
              {recuperacaoPendente && <button onClick={() => iniciarRodadaAutorizada()}>Continuar atualização</button>}
            </div>
            <div className="bolhas" ref={bolhas} aria-live="polite">
              {aberta.messages.map((m: Mensagem) => <div key={m.id} data-message-id={m.id} className={`bolha ${m.direction === 'OUT' ? 'saiu' : ''}`}>
                <div>{m.content}</div>
                <div className="suave">{new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}{m.authorName ? ` · ${m.authorName}` : ''}</div>
              </div>)}
              {aberta.messages.length === 0 && <p className="suave">Nenhuma mensagem nesta conversa.</p>}
            </div>

            {envioIndisponivel && <div className="aviso" role="status">{janelaFechada
              ? 'Fora da janela de 24h do canal selecionado. Texto livre está indisponível.'
              : explicarMotivoEnvio(aberta.janela.motivo)}</div>}
            {requiresInitialFlow && activeConversationId && <InstagramDraftReview actorId={eu.id} buId={buId} conversationId={activeConversationId} />}
            {startOptionsError && aberta.messages.length === 0
              ? <div className="aviso grade" role="status"><span>{startOptionsError} O envio continua bloqueado até uma leitura atual.</span><button type="button" onClick={() => void retryStartOptions(aberta.conversa.id, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)}>Tentar novamente</button></div>
              : aberta.messages.length === 0 && startOptionsLoading
                ? <p className="suave" role="status">Conferindo opções do primeiro envio…</p>
                : requiresInitialFlow
                  ? currentStartOptions
                    ? ordinaryUncertainAdmission && !currentStartOptions.activeAttempt
                      ? <OrdinaryRecovery key={`ordinary-recovery:${eu.id}:${buId}:${aberta.conversa.id}:${activeGeneration}:${activeNavigation}:${ordinaryUncertainAdmission.id}`} conversationId={aberta.conversa.id} admission={ordinaryUncertainAdmission} beginOptionsRead={() => beginStartOptionsRead(eu.id, buId, aberta.conversa.id, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)} isOptionsReadCurrent={startOptionsTicketIsCurrent} invalidateOptionsReads={invalidateStartOptionsReads} onAlert={publicarAlerta} onClearAlert={limparAlerta} onOptions={(options, ticket) => publishStartOptions(ticket, options)} />
                      : <PrimeiraMensagem key={`${eu.id}:${buId}:${aberta.conversa.id}:${activeGeneration}:${activeNavigation}`} actorId={eu.id} buId={buId} conversationId={aberta.conversa.id} onAlert={publicarAlerta} onClearAlert={limparAlerta} options={currentStartOptions} beginOptionsRead={() => beginStartOptionsRead(eu.id, buId, aberta.conversa.id, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)} isOptionsReadCurrent={startOptionsTicketIsCurrent} onOptions={(options, ticket) => publishStartOptions(ticket, options)} onComplete={() => completeInitial(aberta.conversa.id, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)} onCancel={aberta.messages.length > 0 ? () => { if (activeGeneration === geracao.current && activeNavigation === navigationIntent.current) setManualInitialConversationId(null); } : undefined} />
                    : <div className="aviso grade" role="status"><span>{startOptionsError ?? 'Não foi possível conferir se existe uma operação ativa.'} O envio continua bloqueado até uma leitura atual.</span><button type="button" onClick={() => void retryStartOptions(aberta.conversa.id, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)}>Tentar novamente</button></div>
                  : aberta.messages.length === 0 && currentStartOptions?.initialRecipientPlatform !== 'WHATSAPP'
                    ? <span className="suave" role="status">{isInstagramConversation ? 'Esta conversa do Instagram só permite responder a uma mensagem recebida.' : 'O destinatário não tem uma rota WhatsApp canônica confirmada. Não é possível iniciar um envio.'}</span>
                    : activeConversationId && <>
                      <InstagramReplyComposer key={`${eu.id}:${buId}:${activeConversationId}:${activeGeneration}:${activeNavigation}`} actorId={eu.id} buId={buId} conversationId={activeConversationId} admissionBlocked={startOptionsUnresolved || activeOrdinaryAdmission} metadataReconnectEpoch={metadataReconnectEpoch} onAlert={publicarAlerta} onClearAlert={limparAlerta} onOutcomeNeedsReview={() => refreshOrdinaryOptions(activeConversationId, activeGeneration, buId, activeNavigation)} onSent={() => {
                        if (actorAtRender === actorAtual.current && activeGeneration === geracao.current && activeNavigation === navigationIntent.current && activeConversationId === conversaAtual.current) {
                          void carregarLista().catch(ignorarSessaoMorta);
                          reconciliar();
                        }
                      }} />
                      {startOptionsUnresolved && <div className="aviso grade" role="status"><span>{startOptionsError ?? 'Conferindo o estado de envio.'} O envio continua bloqueado até uma leitura atual.</span><button type="button" onClick={() => void retryStartOptions(activeConversationId, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)}>Tentar novamente</button></div>}
                      {activeOrdinaryAdmission && ordinaryUncertainAdmission && <OrdinaryRecovery key={`ordinary-recovery:${eu.id}:${buId}:${aberta.conversa.id}:${activeGeneration}:${activeNavigation}:${ordinaryUncertainAdmission.id}`} conversationId={aberta.conversa.id} admission={ordinaryUncertainAdmission} beginOptionsRead={() => beginStartOptionsRead(eu.id, buId, activeConversationId, activeGeneration, activeNavigation)} isOptionsReadCurrent={startOptionsTicketIsCurrent} invalidateOptionsReads={invalidateStartOptionsReads} onAlert={publicarAlerta} onClearAlert={limparAlerta} onOptions={(options, ticket) => publishStartOptions(ticket, options)} />}
                    </>}
            {aberta.conversa.status !== 'CLOSED' && <div className="linha">
              <button onClick={() => void agir(() => api.post(`/conversations/${aberta.conversa.id}/claim`))}>Atender</button>
              <button onClick={() => void agir(() => api.post(`/conversations/${aberta.conversa.id}/close`))}>Finalizar</button>
            </div>}
          </>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


## FULL SOURCE BODY web-app/src/componentes/NovaConversa.spec.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { TemplateSummary } from '../api';
import { fingerprintInitialTemplate, isInitialSendSnapshot, recoveredAttemptForOptions, type InitialAttempt, type StartOptions } from './NovaConversa';

function summary(overrides: Partial<TemplateSummary> = {}): TemplateSummary {
  return {
    remoteTemplateId: 'remote-1', status: 'APPROVED', name: 'hello_customer', language: 'pt_BR',
    category: 'UTILITY', parameterFormat: 'POSITIONAL',
    components: [
      { type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{2}}, seu pedido {{1}} chegou.', parameters: ['2', '1'] },
      { type: 'FOOTER', text: 'Equipe', parameters: [] },
      { type: 'HEADER', format: 'TEXT', text: 'Atualização', parameters: [] },
    ],
    supported: false, semanticFingerprint: null, available: true, observedAt: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
    observedFence: 'fence-1', eligible: false, ...overrides,
  };
}

function canonical(value: unknown): string {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? `[${value.map(canonical).join(',')}]`
    : value && typeof value === 'object' ? `{${Object.keys(value as Record<string, unknown>).sort()
      .map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key])}`).join(',')}}`
      : JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';
}

function snapshotFor(message: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const body = { channelId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', expectedGeneration: 1,
    mensagem: message, previewFingerprint: 'a'.repeat(64) };
  return { key: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', request: canonical(body), body };
}

describe('fingerprint de modelo para mensagem inicial', () => {
  it('usa a ordem semântica HEADER/BODY/FOOTER e ignora sinalizadores de autoria ST05', async () => {
    const first = summary();
    const second = summary({
      components: [first.components[2], first.components[0], { type: 'BUTTONS', buttons: [{ text: 'Gerenciar' }] }, first.components[1]],
      supported: true, semanticFingerprint: 'authoring-only', available: false, eligible: true,
      observedAt: '2026-10-22T10:05:00.000Z', observedFence: 'new-fence',
    });
    await expect(fingerprintInitialTemplate(first)).resolves.toBe(await fingerprintInitialTemplate(second));
  });

  it('mantém o formato do parâmetro e o conteúdo estático no hash semântico', async () => {
    const base = summary();
    const named = summary({ parameterFormat: 'NAMED' });
    const changedFooter = summary({ components: [base.components[0], { type: 'FOOTER', text: 'Outro rodapé', parameters: [] }, base.components[2]] });
    const fingerprint = await fingerprintInitialTemplate(base);
    await expect(fingerprintInitialTemplate(named)).resolves.not.toBe(fingerprint);
    await expect(fingerprintInitialTemplate(changedFooter)).resolves.not.toBe(fingerprint);
  });
});

describe('restauração segura da tentativa inicial', () => {
  it('applies text and template value limits in Unicode code points, independently of the bounded JSON envelope', () => {
    const textAtLimit = snapshotFor({ tipo: 'TEXTO', texto: '😀'.repeat(4096) });
    const textOverLimit = snapshotFor({ tipo: 'TEXTO', texto: '😀'.repeat(4097) });
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(textAtLimit)).toBe(true);
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(textOverLimit)).toBe(false);

    const valueAtLimit = snapshotFor({ tipo: 'TEMPLATE', templateId: 'remote-template', language: 'pt_BR',
      expectedDefinitionFingerprint: 'b'.repeat(64), parameters: ['😀'.repeat(1024)] });
    const valueOverLimit = snapshotFor({ tipo: 'TEMPLATE', templateId: 'remote-template', language: 'pt_BR',
      expectedDefinitionFingerprint: 'b'.repeat(64), parameters: ['😀'.repeat(1025)] });
    const namedAtLimit = snapshotFor({ tipo: 'TEMPLATE', templateId: 'remote-template', language: 'pt_BR',
      expectedDefinitionFingerprint: 'b'.repeat(64), parameters: { customer: '😀'.repeat(1024) } });
    const namedOverLimit = snapshotFor({ tipo: 'TEMPLATE', templateId: 'remote-template', language: 'pt_BR',
      expectedDefinitionFingerprint: 'b'.repeat(64), parameters: { customer: '😀'.repeat(1025) } });
    expect(valueAtLimit.request.length).toBeLessThan(32_768);
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(valueAtLimit)).toBe(true);
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(valueOverLimit)).toBe(false);
    expect(namedAtLimit.request.length).toBeLessThan(32_768);
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(namedAtLimit)).toBe(true);
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(namedOverLimit)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('preserva o limite completo de texto aceito mesmo quando caracteres JSON são escapados', () => {
    const body = { channelId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', expectedGeneration: 1,
      mensagem: { texto: '\u0001'.repeat(4096), tipo: 'TEXTO' }, previewFingerprint: 'a'.repeat(64) };
    const canonical = (value: unknown): string => Array.isArray(value) ? `[${value.map(canonical).join(',')}]`
      : value && typeof value === 'object' ? `{${Object.keys(value as Record<string, unknown>).sort()
        .map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key])}`).join(',')}}`
        : JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';
    const request = canonical(body);
    expect(request.length).toBeGreaterThan(16_384);
    const snapshot = { key: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', request, body };
    const restored = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(snapshot)) as unknown;
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(restored)).toBe(true);
    expect(restored).toEqual(snapshot);
  });

  it('preserva o limite de renderização do modelo após escaping JSON', () => {
    const body = { channelId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', expectedGeneration: 1,
      mensagem: { tipo: 'TEMPLATE', templateId: 'remote-template', language: 'pt_BR',
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: 'b'.repeat(64), parameters: Array.from({ length: 4 }, () => '\u0001'.repeat(1024)) },
      previewFingerprint: 'a'.repeat(64) };
    const canonical = (value: unknown): string => Array.isArray(value) ? `[${value.map(canonical).join(',')}]`
      : value && typeof value === 'object' ? `{${Object.keys(value as Record<string, unknown>).sort()
        .map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key])}`).join(',')}}`
        : JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';
    const request = canonical(body);
    expect(request.length).toBeGreaterThan(24_000);
    const snapshot = { key: '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333', request, body };
    const restored = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(snapshot)) as unknown;
    expect(isInitialSendSnapshot(restored)).toBe(true);
    expect(restored).toEqual(snapshot);
  });
});

describe('observação local da recuperação do primeiro envio', () => {
  it('retira uma tentativa local quando novas opções aceitas informam activeAttempt nulo', () => {
    const originalOptions: StartOptions = {
      conversationId: 'conversation-1', initialRecipientPlatform: 'WHATSAPP', channels: [], activeAttempt: null,
      ordinaryAdmissions: [], admissionsTruncated: false, recentAttempts: [], attemptsTruncated: false,
    };
    const attempt: InitialAttempt = {
      id: 'attempt-a', state: 'AMBIGUOUS', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
      localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED', recoveryAction: 'ACK_AFTER_DEADLINE', eligibleAt: '2026-10-25T00:00:00.000Z',
      slotReleasedAt: null, riskAcknowledgementAvailable: true,
    };
    const localFact = { attempt, basedOnOptions: originalOptions };
    expect(recoveredAttemptForOptions(localFact, originalOptions)).toBe(attempt);

    const authoritativeNullOptions: StartOptions = { ...originalOptions, activeAttempt: null, recentAttempts: [] };
    expect(authoritativeNullOptions).not.toBe(originalOptions);
    expect(recoveredAttemptForOptions(localFact, authoritativeNullOptions)).toBeNull();
  });
});


## FULL SOURCE BODY web-app/e2e/channel-transport.spec.ts

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { expect, test, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

const FIXTURES = 'http://127.0.0.1:3101';
const FRONTDOOR = 'https://127.0.0.1:4174';

test.use({ baseURL: FRONTDOOR, ignoreHTTPSErrors: true });

async function seed(request: APIRequestContext, includeEvolution = false, count = 3) {
  const response = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/seed`, { data: { count, includeEvolution, requiresWindow: true } });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(201);
  return response.json() as Promise<{
    email: string; password: string; businessUnitId: string; conversationId: string;
    otherConversationId: string; otherBusinessUnitId: string; otherBuConversationId: string; otherBuPhoneNumberId: string; phoneNumberId: string;
  }>;
}

async function holdNativeReply(request: APIRequestContext, conversationId: string) {
  const response = await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/hold`, { data: { conversationId } });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
}

async function pendingNativeReply(request: APIRequestContext) {
  let state: { pending: null | { status: number; bodyHash: string; bodyBytes: number; headers: Record<string, unknown> } } = { pending: null };
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 200 && !state.pending; attempt += 1) {
    const response = await request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/state`);
    expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
    state = await response.json();
    if (!state.pending) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 25));
  }
  expect(state.pending).not.toBeNull();
  return state.pending!;
}

function canonicalRequestBody(value: unknown): string {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalRequestBody).join(',')}]`;
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    const row = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    return `{${Object.keys(row).sort().map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalRequestBody(row[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
  }
  return JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';
}

async function releaseNativeReply(request: APIRequestContext) {
  const response = await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/release`);
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
}

async function loginAndOpen(page: Page, fixture: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof seed>>, messages = 3) {
  await page.context().clearCookies();
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('E-mail').fill(fixture.email);
  await page.getByLabel('Senha').fill(fixture.password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/inbox$/);
  await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('.bolha')).toHaveCount(messages);
}

test.describe('Channel outbound pela Inbox com BFF e PostgreSQL reais', () => {
  test('envia por Meta, mantém rascunho em ACK sem ID e bloqueia conversa ambígua', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    const incoming = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: {
      conversationId: fixture.conversationId, phoneNumberId: fixture.phoneNumberId,
      text: 'entrada oficial assinada e atual', target: 'https-browser',
    } });
    expect(incoming.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await incoming.json()).status).toBe(200);
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 4);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await composer.fill('resposta oficial de teste');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: 'resposta oficial de teste' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('');

    await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/send-mode`, { data: { mode: 'unknown' } });
    await composer.fill('rascunho não reenviado');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('aceitou o envio sem confirmar o ID externo');
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('rascunho não reenviado');

    const ambiguous = await seed(request, true, 0);
    await page.context().clearCookies();
    await page.goto('/login');
    await page.getByLabel('E-mail').fill(ambiguous.email);
    await page.getByLabel('Senha').fill(ambiguous.password);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' }).click();
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(ambiguous.businessUnitId);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText('Esta conversa ainda não tem um canal definido. Aguarde uma mensagem recebida para responder.')).toBeVisible();
    // Empty conversations enter the assisted first-send flow; the ordinary
    // composer is absent until a channel can be selected and the flow resolves.
    await expect(page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test('seleciona explicitamente a rota V2 e descarta resposta atrasada após troca A→B→A', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request, true);
    const incoming = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: {
      conversationId: fixture.conversationId, phoneNumberId: fixture.phoneNumberId,
      text: 'entrada oficial assinada para escolher uma rota atual', target: 'https-browser',
    } });
    expect(incoming.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await incoming.json()).status).toBe(200);
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 4);
    const channelSelect = page.getByLabel('Canal e conta');
    await expect(channelSelect).toBeVisible();
    await expect(channelSelect.locator('option')).toHaveCount(3);
    const routeOptions = await channelSelect.locator('option').evaluateAll((items) => items.map((item) => ({ value: (item as HTMLOptionElement).value, label: item.textContent ?? '' })));
    const evolution = routeOptions.find((item) => /não oficial/i.test(item.label));
    expect(evolution).toBeTruthy();
    await channelSelect.selectOption(evolution!.value);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await composer.fill('resposta não oficial de teste');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: 'resposta não oficial de teste' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('');

    await holdNativeReply(request, fixture.conversationId);
    let released = false;
    try {
      const responsePath = `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`;
    await page.evaluate((pathname) => {
      type BodyReadState = {
        started: number;
        settled: number;
        processed: number;
        completion: Promise<void>;
        resolveCompletion: () => void;
        originalFetch: typeof window.fetch;
      };
      const w = window as typeof window & { __stcon06BodyRead?: BodyReadState };
      let resolveCompletion!: () => void;
      const state: BodyReadState = {
        started: 0,
        settled: 0,
        processed: 0,
        completion: new Promise<void>((resolve) => { resolveCompletion = resolve; }),
        resolveCompletion: () => resolveCompletion(),
        originalFetch: window.fetch,
      };
      const nativeFetch = window.fetch.bind(window);
      window.fetch = async (input, init) => {
        const requestUrl = input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input);
        const method = init?.method ?? (input instanceof Request ? input.method : 'GET');
        const response = await nativeFetch(input, init);
        if (method === 'POST' && new URL(requestUrl, window.location.href).pathname === pathname) {
          const nativeText = response.text.bind(response);
          response.text = () => {
            state.started += 1;
            return nativeText().then((body) => {
              state.settled += 1;
              // A browser task runs after this promise and the awaiting api.ts / responder
              // microtasks, including the generation fence and draft decision.
              window.setTimeout(() => {
                state.processed += 1;
                state.resolveCompletion();
              }, 0);
              return body;
            });
          };
        }
        return response;
      };
      w.__stcon06BodyRead = state;
    }, responsePath);
    await composer.fill('resposta cuja resposta chega tarde');
    const solicitacao = page.waitForRequest((requestEvent) => requestEvent.method() === 'POST' && new URL(requestEvent.url()).pathname === responsePath);
    const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === responsePath);
    void solicitacao.catch(() => undefined);
    void responsePromise.catch(() => undefined);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await solicitacao;
    const pending = await pendingNativeReply(request);
    expect(pending.status).toBe(201);
    expect(pending.bodyBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.otherBusinessUnitId);
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Business Unit')).toHaveValue(fixture.otherBusinessUnitId);
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item')).toHaveCount(1);
    await page.locator('.cartao.lista .item').first().click();
    const activeThreadTitle = page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha > strong');
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).not.toHaveText('Cliente Timeline');
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Secundário' }).click();
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveText('Cliente Secundário');
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item.ativo')).toHaveCount(1);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Em atendimento' }).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveText('Cliente Timeline');
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item.ativo')).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(channelSelect).toBeVisible();
    await expect(channelSelect.locator('option')).toHaveCount(3);
    const currentRoutes = await channelSelect.locator('option').evaluateAll((items) => items.map((item) => ({ value: (item as HTMLOptionElement).value, label: item.textContent ?? '' })));
    const currentEvolution = currentRoutes.find((item) => item.value === evolution!.value && /não oficial/i.test(item.label));
    expect(currentEvolution).toBeTruthy();
    await channelSelect.selectOption(currentEvolution!.value);
    await composer.fill('resposta cuja resposta chega tarde');
      await releaseNativeReply(request);
      released = true;
    const responseAntiga = await responsePromise;
    expect(responseAntiga.status()).toBe(201);
    expect(await responseAntiga.finished()).toBeNull();
    const frontdoorState = await (await request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/state`)).json();
    const responseBody = await responseAntiga.body();
    expect(frontdoorState.completed).toMatchObject({ status: 201, bodyBytes: responseBody.byteLength });
    expect(frontdoorState.completed.bodyHash).toBe(await import('node:crypto').then(({ createHash }) => createHash('sha256').update(responseBody).digest('hex')));
    expect(frontdoorState.completed.headers['content-type']).toContain('application/json');
    const bodyRead = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const state = (window as typeof window & { __stcon06BodyRead?: {
        completion: Promise<void>; started: number; settled: number; processed: number; originalFetch: typeof window.fetch;
      } }).__stcon06BodyRead;
      if (!state) throw new Error('The response body read instrumentation was not installed.');
      await state.completion;
      window.fetch = state.originalFetch;
      return { started: state.started, settled: state.settled, processed: state.processed };
    });
    expect(bodyRead).toEqual({ started: 1, settled: 1, processed: 1 });
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('resposta cuja resposta chega tarde');
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toHaveCount(0);
    } finally {
      if (!released) await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/release`).catch(() => undefined);
      await page.evaluate(() => {
        const state = (window as typeof window & { __stcon06BodyRead?: { originalFetch: typeof window.fetch } }).__stcon06BodyRead;
        if (state) window.fetch = state.originalFetch;
      }).catch(() => undefined);
    }
  });

  test('repete opções após uma falha atrasada atravessar uma reconexão real do socket', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    const incoming = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: {
      conversationId: fixture.conversationId, phoneNumberId: fixture.phoneNumberId,
      text: 'entrada qualificada para a conversa atual', target: 'https-browser',
    } });
    expect(incoming.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await incoming.json()).status).toBe(200);

    const providerHeaders = { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' };
    const providerBeforeResponse = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: providerHeaders });
    expect(providerBeforeResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const sendsBefore = ((await providerBeforeResponse.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string }>).filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.send').length;

    const connectedSockets = new Set<import('@playwright/test').WebSocket>();
    const closedSockets = new Set<import('@playwright/test').WebSocket>();
    page.on('websocket', (socket) => {
      if (socket.url() !== 'wss://127.0.0.1:4174/ws') return;
      socket.on('close', () => closedSockets.add(socket));
      socket.on('framereceived', (frame) => {
        try {
          const event: unknown = JSON.parse(typeof frame.payload === 'string' ? frame.payload : frame.payload.toString());
          if (event && typeof event === 'object' && !Array.isArray(event)
            && (event as { tipo?: unknown }).tipo === 'conectado'
            && Array.isArray((event as { escopo?: unknown }).escopo)
            && (event as { escopo: string[] }).escopo.includes(fixture.businessUnitId)) connectedSockets.add(socket);
        } catch { /* Ignore non-JSON websocket frames. */ }
      });
    });

    let resolveFirstOptionsHeld!: () => void;
    const firstOptionsHeld = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { resolveFirstOptionsHeld = resolve; });
    let abortFirstOptions!: () => void;
    const abortFirstOptionsRequest = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { abortFirstOptions = resolve; });
    let optionsRequests = 0;
    const optionsRoute = `**/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/start-options`;
    let firstOptionsRouteCallbackFinished: Promise<void> | null = null;
    let finishFirstOptionsRouteCallback: (() => void) | null = null;
    const optionsRouteHandler = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      optionsRequests += 1;
      if (optionsRequests === 1) {
        firstOptionsRouteCallbackFinished = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { finishFirstOptionsRouteCallback = resolve; });
        resolveFirstOptionsHeld();
        try {
          await abortFirstOptionsRequest;
          await route.abort('failed');
        } catch (error) {
          if (route.request().failure()?.errorText !== 'net::ERR_ABORTED') throw error;
        } finally {
          finishFirstOptionsRouteCallback?.();
        }
        return;
      }
      await route.continue();
    };
    await page.route(optionsRoute, optionsRouteHandler);

    let routeReleased = false;
    try {
      await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 4);
      await firstOptionsHeld;
      await expect.poll(() => connectedSockets.size).toBe(1);
      const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
      await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: /Conferindo/ })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(composer).toHaveCount(0);

      const socketDropResponse = await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/drop-wss`, {
        headers: { 'x-fixture-wss-control': 'native109-raw-wss-close-only' },
      });
      expect(socketDropResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      expect(await socketDropResponse.json()).toEqual({ closed: 1 });
      await expect.poll(() => closedSockets.size).toBe(1);
      await expect.poll(() => connectedSockets.size, { timeout: 15_000 }).toBeGreaterThan(1);

      const retryResponsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/start-options`);
      abortFirstOptions();
      routeReleased = true;
      const retryResponse = await retryResponsePromise;
      expect(retryResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      expect(optionsRequests).toBe(2);

      await expect(page.getByLabel('Business Unit')).toHaveValue(fixture.businessUnitId);
      await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item.ativo')).toContainText('Cliente Timeline');
      await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
      await composer.fill('rascunho digitado após a recuperação');
      await expect(composer).toHaveValue('rascunho digitado após a recuperação');
      expect(await page.getByRole('alert').allTextContents()).toEqual([]);

      const providerAfterResponse = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: providerHeaders });
      expect(providerAfterResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      const sendsAfter = ((await providerAfterResponse.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string }>).filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.send').length;
      expect(sendsAfter).toBe(sendsBefore);
      const auditResponse = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/audit-outbound?conversationId=${encodeURIComponent(fixture.conversationId)}&content=${encodeURIComponent('rascunho digitado após a recuperação')}`);
      expect(auditResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      expect((await auditResponse.json()).total).toBe(0);
    } finally {
      if (!routeReleased) {
        abortFirstOptions();
        routeReleased = true;
      }
      if (firstOptionsRouteCallbackFinished) await firstOptionsRouteCallbackFinished;
      await page.unroute(optionsRoute, optionsRouteHandler);
    }
  });

  test('bloqueia vinte novos submits durante cada envio real em cinco ondas', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    const incoming = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: {
      conversationId: fixture.conversationId, phoneNumberId: fixture.phoneNumberId,
      text: 'entrada oficial assinada e atual para as cinco ondas', target: 'https-browser',
    } });
    expect(incoming.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await incoming.json()).status).toBe(200);
    for (const [conversationId, phoneNumberId, text] of [
      [fixture.otherConversationId, fixture.phoneNumberId, 'entrada secundária para onda isolada'],
      [fixture.otherBuConversationId, fixture.otherBuPhoneNumberId, 'entrada alternativa para onda isolada'],
    ]) {
      const received = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: { conversationId, phoneNumberId, text, target: 'https-browser' } });
      expect(received.status()).toBe(200);
      expect((await received.json()).status).toBe(200);
    }
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 4);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    const providerStateResponse = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' } });
    expect(providerStateResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const providerCallsAtStart = (await providerStateResponse.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string }>;
    const sendsAtStart = providerCallsAtStart.filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.send').length;
    const waveEvidence: Array<{ requests: number; retriggers: number; outcome: 'accepted' | 'unknown'; draftPreserved: boolean; messageCount: number }> = [];
    const form = page.locator('form').filter({ has: composer });
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) {
      const outcome = round % 2 === 1 ? 'unknown' : 'accepted';
      const conversationId = round === 1 ? fixture.otherConversationId : round === 3 ? fixture.otherBuConversationId : fixture.conversationId;
      if (round === 1) {
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Na fila' }).click();
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Secundário' }).click();
        await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
      } else if (round === 2 || round === 4) {
        await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Em atendimento' }).click();
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
        await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
      } else if (round === 3) {
        await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.otherBusinessUnitId);
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Na fila' }).click();
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Outra BU' }).click();
        await expect(composer).toBeVisible();
      }
      const text = `composer wave ${round} ${outcome}`;
      await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/send-mode`, { data: { mode: outcome } });
      let requestCount = 0;
      let actualStatus = 0;
      await holdNativeReply(request, conversationId);
      let released = false;
      let observeRequest: ((requestEvent: import('@playwright/test').Request) => void) | undefined;
      let responseObserver: ReturnType<Page['waitForResponse']> | undefined;
      try {
        const responsePath = `/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`;
        const outgoingRequests: string[] = [];
        observeRequest = (requestEvent: import('@playwright/test').Request) => {
          if (requestEvent.method() === 'POST' && new URL(requestEvent.url()).pathname === responsePath) { requestCount += 1; outgoingRequests.push(requestEvent.url()); }
        };
        page.on('request', observeRequest);
        await composer.fill(text);
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
        const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST' && new URL(response.url()).pathname === responsePath);
        responseObserver = responsePromise;
        void responsePromise.catch(() => undefined);
        await pendingNativeReply(request);
        const state = await (await request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/state`)).json();
        actualStatus = state.pending.status;
        for (let index = 0; index < 20; index += 1) {
          await form.evaluate((element) => element.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true })));
        }
        expect(requestCount).toBe(1);
        await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviando…' })).toBeDisabled();
        await releaseNativeReply(request);
        released = true;
        const completedResponse = await responsePromise;
        expect(completedResponse.status()).toBe(actualStatus);
        if (outcome === 'unknown') {
          await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('aceitou o envio sem confirmar o ID externo');
          await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
          expect(actualStatus).toBe(409);
        } else {
          await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: text })).toBeVisible();
          await expect(composer).toHaveValue('');
          expect(actualStatus).toBe(201);
        }
        const auditResponse = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/audit-outbound?conversationId=${encodeURIComponent(conversationId)}&content=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`);
        expect(auditResponse.status()).toBe(200);
        const audit = await auditResponse.json() as { total: number; accepted: number; failed: number; failedWithoutExternalId: number };
        expect(audit).toEqual(outcome === 'unknown'
          ? { total: 1, accepted: 0, failed: 1, failedWithoutExternalId: 1 }
          : { total: 1, accepted: 1, failed: 0, failedWithoutExternalId: 0 });
        const providerState = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' } });
        expect(providerState.status()).toBe(200);
        const providerCalls = (await providerState.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string }>;
        expect(providerCalls.filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.send')).toHaveLength(sendsAtStart + round + 1);
        waveEvidence.push({ requests: requestCount, retriggers: 20, outcome, draftPreserved: outcome === 'unknown', messageCount: audit.total });
        if (outcome === 'unknown') {
          await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Atualizar opções' }).click();
          await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
          await expect(composer).toBeDisabled();
          await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar', exact: true })).toBeDisabled();
        }
      } finally {
        if (observeRequest) page.off('request', observeRequest);
        if (!released) await request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/__test/fcr/release`).catch(() => undefined);
        if (responseObserver) void responseObserver.catch(() => undefined);
      }
    }
    expect(waveEvidence).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(waveEvidence.every((wave) => wave.requests === 1 && wave.retriggers === 20)).toBe(true);
    expect(waveEvidence.filter((wave) => wave.outcome === 'accepted').every((wave) => wave.messageCount === 1 && !wave.draftPreserved)).toBe(true);
    expect(waveEvidence.filter((wave) => wave.outcome === 'unknown').every((wave) => wave.messageCount === 1 && wave.draftPreserved)).toBe(true);
    console.log(`[FRC-COMPOSER-EVIDENCE]${JSON.stringify({ waves: waveEvidence, limits: 'Five real BFF/PostgreSQL submits held after the actual response; twenty user-form submit events per in-flight send; one request per wave; no capacity/SLO claim.' })}`);
  });

  test('preserves the 4,096 WhatsApp code-point boundary for supplementary-plane input', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request, false, 0);
    const incoming = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: {
      conversationId: fixture.conversationId, phoneNumberId: fixture.phoneNumberId,
      text: 'entrada para limite Unicode', target: 'https-browser',
    } });
    expect(incoming.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await incoming.json()).status).toBe(200);
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 1);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await composer.fill('😀'.repeat(4096));
    expect(await composer.evaluate((element) => [...(element as HTMLTextAreaElement).value].length)).toBe(4096);
    await composer.fill('😀'.repeat(4097));
    expect(await composer.evaluate((element) => [...(element as HTMLTextAreaElement).value].length)).toBe(4096);
    const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    const response = await responsePromise;
    expect(response.status()).toBe(201);
    const audit = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/audit?conversationId=${encodeURIComponent(fixture.conversationId)}`);
    expect(audit.status()).toBe(200);
    const persisted = (await audit.json()).messages as Array<{ direction: string; content: string }>;
    expect(persisted.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ direction: 'OUT', content: '😀'.repeat(4096) });
  });

  test('isolates a replacement ordinary admission while the older real reconciliation response is held', async ({ page, request }) => {
    test.setTimeout(180_000);
    const fixture = await seed(request, false, 0);
    const incoming = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/incoming`, { data: {
      conversationId: fixture.conversationId, phoneNumberId: fixture.phoneNumberId,
      text: 'entrada para substituir uma admissão comum', target: 'https-browser',
    } });
    expect(incoming.status()).toBe(200);
    expect((await incoming.json()).status).toBe(200);

    const replyOptionsPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/reply-options`;
    const replyOptionsWait = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === replyOptionsPath);
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 1);
    const replyOptionsResponse = await replyOptionsWait;
    expect(replyOptionsResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const replyOptions = await replyOptionsResponse.json() as { options: Array<{
      routeId: string; channelId: string; generation: number; contextFingerprint: string; available: boolean; supportedKinds: string[];
    }> };
    const selected = replyOptions.options.find((option) => option.available && option.supportedKinds.includes('text'));
    expect(selected).toBeTruthy();
    const providerBefore = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' } });
    expect(providerBefore.status()).toBe(200);
    const sendsBefore = ((await providerBefore.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string }>).filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.send').length;

    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/send-mode`, { data: { mode: 'unknown' } });
    const firstText = 'primeiro envio comum sem confirmação';
    const firstSend = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`);
    const firstOptions = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/start-options`);
    await composer.fill(firstText);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    expect((await firstSend).status()).toBe(409);
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue(firstText);
    const firstOptionsResponse = await firstOptions;
    expect(firstOptionsResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const firstSnapshot = await firstOptionsResponse.json() as { ordinaryAdmissions: Array<{
      id: string; state: string; dispatchDeadlineAt: string | null;
    }> };
    const admissionA = firstSnapshot.ordinaryAdmissions.find((item) => ['SENDING', 'UNCERTAIN'].includes(item.state));
    expect(admissionA?.id).toBeTruthy();
    expect(Date.parse(admissionA!.dispatchDeadlineAt ?? '')).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());
    await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recuperação do envio comum' })).toBeVisible();
    const riskCheckbox = page.getByLabel('Confirmo o risco de mensagem duplicada.');
    await riskCheckbox.check();
    await expect(riskCheckbox).toBeChecked();

    const pathA = `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/dispatch-admissions/${admissionA!.id}/reconcile`;
    let releaseA: (() => void) | null = null;
    let signalA: (() => void) | null = null;
    const heldA = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { signalA = resolve; });
    const releaseAWait = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseA = resolve; });
    let aStatus = 0;
    let aBodyHash = '';
    let aBodyBytes = 0;
    let aRouteInvoked = false;
    let finishAHandler: (() => void) | null = null;
    const aHandlerFinished = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { finishAHandler = resolve; });
    const holdA = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      aRouteInvoked = true;
      try {
        const actual = await route.fetch();
        const body = await actual.body();
        aStatus = actual.status();
        aBodyBytes = body.byteLength;
        aBodyHash = createHash('sha256').update(body).digest('hex');
        signalA?.();
        await releaseAWait;
        await route.fulfill({ response: actual });
      } finally { finishAHandler?.(); }
    };
    await page.route(`**${pathA}**`, holdA);
    let signalB: (() => void) | null = null;
    const heldB = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { signalB = resolve; });
    let bStatus = 0;
    let bBodyHash = '';
    let bBodyBytes = 0;
    let bRouteInvoked = false;
    let finishBHandler: (() => void) | null = null;
    const bHandlerFinished = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { finishBHandler = resolve; });
    const startOptionsPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/start-options`;
    let releaseB: (() => void) | null = null;
    const releaseBWait = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseB = resolve; });
    const holdBOptions = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      bRouteInvoked = true;
      try {
        const actual = await route.fetch();
        const body = await actual.body();
        bStatus = actual.status();
        bBodyBytes = body.byteLength;
        bBodyHash = createHash('sha256').update(body).digest('hex');
        signalB?.();
        await releaseBWait;
        await route.fulfill({ response: actual });
      } finally { finishBHandler?.(); }
    };
    let metadataRequests = 0;
    const countMetadataRequests = (requestEvent: import('@playwright/test').Request) => {
      if (requestEvent.method() === 'GET' && new URL(requestEvent.url()).pathname === startOptionsPath) metadataRequests += 1;
    };
    await page.evaluate((pathname) => {
      type CompletionState = { processed: boolean; originalFetch: typeof window.fetch };
      const w = window as typeof window & { __ordinaryACompletion?: CompletionState };
      const state: CompletionState = { processed: false, originalFetch: window.fetch };
      const nativeFetch = window.fetch.bind(window);
      window.fetch = async (input, init) => {
        const requestUrl = input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input);
        const method = init?.method ?? (input instanceof Request ? input.method : 'GET');
        const response = await nativeFetch(input, init);
        if (method === 'POST' && new URL(requestUrl, window.location.href).pathname === pathname) {
          const nativeText = response.text.bind(response);
          response.text = () => nativeText().then((body) => {
            window.setTimeout(() => { state.processed = true; }, 0);
            return body;
          });
        }
        return response;
      };
      w.__ordinaryACompletion = state;
    }, pathA);
    let acknowledgePosts = 0;
    const countAcknowledge = (requestEvent: import('@playwright/test').Request) => {
      if (requestEvent.method() === 'POST' && /\/dispatch-admissions\/[^/]+\/acknowledge$/.test(new URL(requestEvent.url()).pathname)) acknowledgePosts += 1;
    };
    page.on('request', countAcknowledge);
    try {
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Consultar estado' }).click();
      await heldA;
      expect(aStatus).toBe(200);
      expect(aBodyBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(aBodyHash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Consultar estado' })).toBeDisabled();

      const secondText = 'segundo envio comum concorrente';
      const secondResponse = await page.context().request.post(`${FRONTDOOR}/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`, {
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-requested-with': 'leadshug-web' },
        data: { schemaVersion: 2, routeId: selected!.routeId, channelId: selected!.channelId,
          expectedGeneration: selected!.generation, contextFingerprint: selected!.contextFingerprint, texto: secondText },
      });
      expect(secondResponse.status()).toBe(409);

      const replacementOptions = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/start-options`);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Atualizar opções' }).click();
      const replacementResponse = await replacementOptions;
      expect(replacementResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      const replacementSnapshot = await replacementResponse.json() as { ordinaryAdmissions: Array<{
        id: string; state: string; dispatchDeadlineAt: string | null;
      }> };
      expect(replacementSnapshot.ordinaryAdmissions.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
      const admissionB = replacementSnapshot.ordinaryAdmissions.find((item) => item.id !== admissionA!.id
        && ['SENDING', 'UNCERTAIN'].includes(item.state));
      expect(admissionB?.id).toBeTruthy();
      expect(Date.parse(admissionB!.dispatchDeadlineAt ?? '')).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());
      await page.route(`**${startOptionsPath}`, holdBOptions);
      await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recuperação do envio comum' })).toBeVisible();
      await expect(riskCheckbox).not.toBeChecked();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Consultar estado' })).toBeEnabled();

      page.on('request', countMetadataRequests);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Consultar estado' }).click();
      await heldB;
      expect(bStatus).toBe(200);
      expect(bBodyBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(bBodyHash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Consultar estado' })).toBeDisabled();
      const metadataCountWithBReadHeld = metadataRequests;
      expect(metadataCountWithBReadHeld).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      const completedA = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === pathA);
      releaseA?.();
      const responseA = await completedA;
      expect(responseA.status()).toBe(aStatus);
      expect(await responseA.finished()).toBeNull();
      const deliveredABody = await responseA.body();
      expect(deliveredABody.byteLength).toBe(aBodyBytes);
      expect(createHash('sha256').update(deliveredABody).digest('hex')).toBe(aBodyHash);
      await aHandlerFinished;
      await page.waitForFunction(() => Boolean((window as typeof window & { __ordinaryACompletion?: { processed: boolean } }).__ordinaryACompletion?.processed));
      expect(metadataRequests).toBe(metadataCountWithBReadHeld);
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Consultar estado' })).toBeDisabled();
      await expect(riskCheckbox).not.toBeChecked();
      await expect(page.getByText('Estado atualizado sem repetir o envio.')).toHaveCount(0);

      const completedB = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
        && new URL(response.url()).pathname === startOptionsPath);
      releaseB?.();
      const responseB = await completedB;
      expect(responseB.status()).toBe(bStatus);
      expect(await responseB.finished()).toBeNull();
      const deliveredBBody = await responseB.body();
      expect(deliveredBBody.byteLength).toBe(bBodyBytes);
      expect(createHash('sha256').update(deliveredBBody).digest('hex')).toBe(bBodyHash);
      await bHandlerFinished;
      await page.evaluate(() => new Promise<void>((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, 0)));
      await expect(page.getByText('Estado atualizado sem repetir o envio.')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(riskCheckbox).not.toBeChecked();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconhecer risco sem reenviar' })).toBeDisabled();
      await riskCheckbox.check();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconhecer risco sem reenviar' })).toBeEnabled({ timeout: 120_000 });
      await riskCheckbox.uncheck();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconhecer risco sem reenviar' })).toBeDisabled();
      expect(acknowledgePosts).toBe(0);

      const providerAfter = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' } });
      expect(providerAfter.status()).toBe(200);
      const sendsAfter = ((await providerAfter.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string }>).filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.send').length;
      expect(sendsAfter).toBe(sendsBefore + 2);
      expect(aStatus).toBe(200);
      expect(bStatus).toBe(200);
    } finally {
      releaseA?.();
      releaseB?.();
      page.off('request', countAcknowledge);
      page.off('request', countMetadataRequests);
      if (aRouteInvoked) await aHandlerFinished;
      if (bRouteInvoked) await bHandlerFinished;
      await page.unroute(`**${pathA}**`, holdA);
      await page.unroute(`**${startOptionsPath}`, holdBOptions);
      await page.evaluate(() => {
        const state = (window as typeof window & { __ordinaryACompletion?: { originalFetch: typeof window.fetch } }).__ordinaryACompletion;
        if (state) window.fetch = state.originalFetch;
      });
    }
  });

  test('discards an older first-send consultation after a real risk release and fresh observation', async ({ page, request }) => {
    test.setTimeout(180_000);
    const fixtureResponse = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/seed`, { data: { count: 0, includeEvolution: false, requiresWindow: false } });
    expect(fixtureResponse.status()).toBe(201);
    const fixture = await fixtureResponse.json() as Awaited<ReturnType<typeof seed>>;
    const providerHeaders = { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' };
    const providerBefore = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/provider/state`, { headers: providerHeaders });
    expect(providerBefore.status()).toBe(200);
    const beforeCalls = (await providerBefore.json()).calls as Array<{ operation: string; phoneNumberId?: string }>;
    const sendsBefore = beforeCalls.filter((call) => call.operation === 'meta.initial.send' && call.phoneNumberId === fixture.phoneNumberId).length;

    await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/provider/config`, {
      headers: { 'x-fixture-provider-token': 'timeline-provider-port-only' }, data: { meta: { sendMode: 'unknown' } },
    }).then((response) => expect(response.status()).toBe(200));
    await page.context().clearCookies();
    await page.goto('/login');
    await page.getByLabel('E-mail').fill(fixture.email);
    await page.getByLabel('Senha').fill(fixture.password);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/inbox$/);
    const meResponse = await page.context().request.get(`${FRONTDOOR}/api/bff/me`);
    expect(meResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const actorId = (await meResponse.json() as { id: string }).id;
    expect(actorId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i);
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Nova conversa' }).click();
    await page.getByLabel('Telefone com DDI explícito').fill('+1 (415) 555-0135');
    const openedResponse = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/business-units/${fixture.businessUnitId}/conversations/open`);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Abrir ou reutilizar' }).click();
    const opened = await openedResponse;
    expect(opened.status()).toBe(201);
    const { conversaId } = await opened.json() as { conversaId: string };
    const channel = await page.getByLabel('Canal selecionado').locator('option').nth(1).getAttribute('value');
    expect(channel).toBeTruthy();
    await page.getByLabel('Canal selecionado').selectOption(channel!);
    await page.getByLabel('Mensagem inicial').fill('primeiro envio ambiguo para ordenar consultas');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Pré-visualizar' }).click();
    const initialPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/initial-messages`;
    const initialPost = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === initialPath);
    const initialOptions = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'GET'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/start-options`);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirmar primeiro envio' }).click();
    const ambiguousResponse = await initialPost;
    expect(ambiguousResponse.status()).toBe(409);
    const ambiguous = await ambiguousResponse.json() as { attemptId: string; state: string };
    expect(ambiguous.state).toBe('AMBIGUOUS');
    expect(ambiguous.attemptId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i);
    const firstOptionsResponse = await initialOptions;
    expect(firstOptionsResponse.status()).toBe(200);
    const firstOptions = await firstOptionsResponse.json() as { activeAttempt: {
      id: string; state: string; eligibleAt: string | null; slotReleasedAt: string | null;
    } | null };
    expect(firstOptions.activeAttempt).toMatchObject({ id: ambiguous.attemptId, state: 'AMBIGUOUS', slotReleasedAt: null });
    expect(Date.parse(firstOptions.activeAttempt!.eligibleAt ?? '')).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());

    const attemptPath = `/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/initial-messages/${ambiguous.attemptId}`;
    let releaseOldRead: (() => void) | null = null;
    let signalOldRead: (() => void) | null = null;
    const oldReadHeld = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { signalOldRead = resolve; });
    const oldReadGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseOldRead = resolve; });
    let oldReadStatus = 0;
    let oldReadHash = '';
    let oldReadBytes = 0;
    let oldReadAttempt: { id: string; state: string; slotReleasedAt: string | null } | null = null;
    let oldReadRouteInvoked = false;
    let finishOldReadRoute: (() => void) | null = null;
    const oldReadRouteFinished = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { finishOldReadRoute = resolve; });
    const holdOldRead = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      if (oldReadRouteInvoked) return route.continue();
